# Onkelos Leviticus

תרגום אונקלוס על ויקרא

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus)

התרגום המרכזי לתורה שהתקבל בתלמוד כתרגום סמכותי; בתקופת התלמוד נקרא בבית הכנסת לצד קריאת התורה, ועדיין נקרא היום בקהילות יהודי תימן.

Primary Targum on the Torah accepted in the Talmud as authoritative; read publicly in synagogues in talmudic times and still today by Yemenite Jews.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Targum/Onkelos/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sifsei Chachomim Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 (Segments: 859, License: CC-BY-NC)

Version (En): Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 [with Onkelos translation] (Segments: 859)


## Chapter 1

###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:1)


וּקְרָא לְמשֶׁה וּמַלֵּיל יְיָ עִמֵּיהּ מִמַּשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְמֵימָר:


And He called to Moshe, and Adonoy spoke to him from the Tent of Meeting, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:2)


מַלֵּיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יְקָרֵיב מִנְּכוֹן קֻרְבָּנָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ מִן בְּעִירָא מִן תּוֹרֵי וּמִן עָנָא תְּקָרְבוּן יָת קֻרְבַּנְכוֹן:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them; [if] a man among you will bring an offering to [**before**] Adonoy; of animals, cattle or sheep you should bring your offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:3)


אִם עֲלָתָא קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ מִן תּוֹרֵי דְּכַר שְׁלִים יְקָרְבִנֵּיהּ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא יְקָרֵב יָתֵיהּ לְרַעֲוָא לֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ:


If his offering is a burnt-offering of cattle he shall bring an unblemished male; to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting he shall bring it, that it be favorably accepted from him before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:4)


וְיִסְמוֹךְ יְדֵיהּ עַל רֵישׁ עֲלָתָא וְיִתְרְעֵי לֵיהּ לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹהִי:


He shall lay his hand on the head of the burnt-offering and it will be favorably accepted from him to achieve his atonement.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:5)


וְיִכּוֹס יָת בַּר תּוֹרֵי קֳדָם יְיָ וִיקָרְבוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא יָת דְּמָא וְיִזְרְקוּן יָת דְּמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר דִּי בִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


He shall slaughter the calf before Adonoy and the sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* will bring the blood and they will sprinkle the blood all around the altar, which is at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:6)


וְיַשְׁלַח יָת עֲלָתָא וִיפַלֵּג יָתַהּ **לְאֶבְרָהָא**:


He shall skin the burnt-offering and cut it into its pieces [**limbs**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:7)


וְיִתְּנוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא אֶשָּׁתָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא וִיסַדְּרוּן אָעַיָּא עַל אֶשָּׁתָא:


The sons of Aharon the *kohein* shall place [ignite] a fire on the altar and they shall arrange logs on the fire.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:8)


וִיסַדְּרוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא יָת **אֶבְרַיָּא** יָת רֵישָׁא וְיָת תַּרְבָּא עַל אָעַיָּא דִּי עַל אֶשָׁתָא דִּי עַל מַדְבְּחָא:


The sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* shall arrange the pieces [**limbs**], the head and the fat on the logs which are on the fire that is on the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:9)


וְגַוֵיהּ וּכְרָעוֹהִי יְחַלֵּל בְּמַיָּא וְיַסֵּק כַּהֲנָא יָת כּוֹלָא לְמַדְבְּחָא עֲלָתָא **קֻרְבַּן דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


He shall wash its innards and its legs in water; and the *kohein* shall burn it all on the altar [for the purpose of] a burnt-offering, a fire-offering [**an offering**] of pleasing fragrance to [**which is accepted with favor before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:10)


וְאִם מִן עָנָא קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ מִן אִמְּרַיָּא אוֹ מִן בְּנֵי עִזַיָּא לַעֲלָתָא דְּכַר שְׁלִים יְקָרְבִינֵיהּ:


If his offering is from flocks of sheep—of lambs or of goats—for a burnt-offering, he shall bring an unblemished male.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:11)


וְיִכּוֹס יָתֵיהּ עַל צִדָּא דְמַדְבְּחָא צִפּוּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ וְיִזְרְקוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא יָת דְּמֵיהּ עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He shall slaughter it on the north side of the altar before Adonoy; and the sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* will sprinkle its blood all around the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:12)


וִיפַלֵּג יָתֵיהּ **לְאֵבָרוֹהִי** וְיָת רֵישֵׁיהּ וְיָת תַּרְבֵּיהּ וְיַסְדַּר כַּהֲנָא יָתְהוֹן עַל אָעַיָּא דִּי עַל אֶשָּׁתָא דִּי עַל מַדְבְּחָא:


He shall sever it into its pieces [**limbs**]—and its head, and [remove] its fats; and the *kohein* shall arrange them on the logs which are on the fire that is on the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:13)


וְגַוָּא וּכְרָעַיָּא יְחַלֵּל בְּמַיָּא וִיקָרֵב כַּהֲנָא יָת כּוֹלָא וְיַסֵּק לְמַדְבְּחָא עֲלָתָא הוּא **קֻרְבַּן דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


He shall wash the innards and feet in water and the *kohein* shall bring it all and burn it on the altar. It is a burnt-offering; a fire-offering [**an offering**] of pleasing fragrance to [**which is accepted with favor before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:14)


וְאִם מִן עוֹפָא עֲלָתָא קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ וִיקָרֵב מִן שַׁפְנִינַיָּא אוֹ מִן בְּנֵי יוֹנָה יָת קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ:


If his burnt-offering to [**before**] Adonoy is of fowl, he shall bring either turtledoves or the young pigeons [as] his offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:15)


וִיקָרְבִנֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא לְמַדְבְּחָא וְיִמְלוֹק יָת רֵישֵׁיהּ וְיַסֵּק לְמַדְבְּחָא וְיִתִּמְצֵי דְמֵיהּ עַל כּוֹתֶל מַדְבְּחָא:


The *kohein* shall bring it near the altar and nip off its head [with his fingernail] and burn it on the altar; and its blood shall be squeezed out onto the wall of the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:16)


וְיַעֲדֵּי יָת זְפָקֵיהּ בְּ**אוּכְלֵיהּ** וְיִרְמֵי יָתַהּ לִסְטַר מַדְבְּחָא קִידוּמָא לַאֲתַר **דְּמוֹשְׁדִין** קִטְמָא:


He shall remove its crop [together] with its feathers [**excrement**] and throw it beside the altar, eastward, to the place of [**where they throw**] the ashes.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/1:17)


וִיפָרֵיק יָתֵיהּ בְּגַדְפוֹהִי לָא יַפְרֵישׁ וְיַסֵּק יָתֵיהּ כַּהֲנָא לְמַדְבְּחָא עַל אָעַיָּא דִּי עַל אֶשָּׁתָא עֲלָתָא הוּא **קֻרְבָּן דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


He shall rip it apart by its wings, not completely dividing it. The *kohein* shall burn it on the altar, on the logs which are on the fire; it is a burnt-offering, a fire-offering [**an offering**] of pleasing fragrance to [**which is accepted with favor before**] Adonoy.



## Chapter 2

###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:1)


וֶאֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יְקָרֵב קֻרְבַּן מִנְחָתָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ סוּלְתָּא יְהֵי קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ וִירִיק עֲלָהּ מִשְׁחָא וְיִתֵּן עֲלָהּ לְבוֹנְתָּא:


When a person brings a meal-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, his offering shall be of fine flour; he shall pour oil upon it and place frankincense upon it.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:2)


וְיַיְתִנָּהּ לְוָת בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא וְיִקְמוֹץ מִתַּמָּן מְלֵי קֻמְצֵיהּ מִסֻּלְתָּא וּמִמִּשְׁחַהּ עַל כָּל לְבוֹנְתָּהּ וְיַסֵּק כַּהֲנָא יָת אַדְכַּרְתָּהּ לְמַדְבְּחָא **קֻרְבַּן דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


He shall bring it to the sons of Aharon, the *kohanim*; from there, [a *kohein*] takes his fistful—from its flour and oil—with all its frankincense. The *kohein* shall burn its memorial portion on the altar, a fire-offering [**an offering**] of pleasing fragrance to [**which is accepted with favor before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:3)


וּדְאִשְׁתָּאַר מִן מִנְחָתָא לְאַהֲרֹן וְלִבְנוֹהִי קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין מִ**קֻּרְבָּנַיָּא** דַיְיָ:


Whatever remains of the meal-offering belongs to Aharon and his sons; [it is] holy of holies of the fire-offerings [**offerings**] of Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:4)


וַאֲרֵי תְּקָרֵיב קֻרְבַּן מִנְחָתָא מַאֲפֵה תַנּוּרָא סוּלְתָּא גְּרִיצָן פַּטִּירָן דְּפִילָן בִּמְשַׁח וְאַסְפּוֹגִין פַּטִּירִין דִּמְשִׁיחִין בִּמְשָׁח:


And when you bring a meal-offering that has been baked in an oven, [it shall be unleavened] matzoh-loaves of fine flour mixed with oil, or [unleavened] matzoh wafers smeared with oil.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:5)


וְאִם מִנְחָתָא עַל מַסְרֵיתָא קֻרְבָּנָךְ סוּלְתָּא דְּפִילָא בִמְשַׁח פַּטִּיר תְּהֵי:


If your offering is a pan-baked meal offering, it shall be of fine flour mixed with oil; it shall be [unleavened] matzoh.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:6)


בְּצַע יָתָהּ בִּיצוּעִין וּתְרִיק עֲלָהּ מִשְׁחָא מִנְחָתָא הִיא:


Break it into pieces and pour oil on it; it is a meal-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:7)


וְאִם מִנְחָתָא **דְרַדְתָּא** קֻרְבָּנָךְ סוּלְתָּא בִמְשַׁח תִּתְעֲבֵד:


If your offering is a deep-pan [**which appears as if it has been ploughed out**] meal offering, you shall make it with fine flour and oil.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:8)


וְתַיְתֵי יָת מִנְחָתָא דִּי יִתְעֲבֵד מֵאִלֵּין **קֳדָם** יְיָ וִיקַרְבִינָה לְוָת כַּהֲנָא וִיקַרְבִנַּהּ לְמַדְבְּחָא:


You shall bring the meal-offering that will be made in any of the aforementioned ways to [**before**] Adonoy. He shall bring it to the *kohein* and he will present it to the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:9)


וְיַפְרֵישׁ כַּהֲנָא מִן מִנְחָתָא יָת אַדְכַּרְתָּה וְיַסֵּק לְמַדְבְּחָא **קֻרְבַּן דְּמִתְקַבֵּל בְּרַעֲוָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


The *kohein* shall separate from the meal-offering its memorial portion and burn it on the altar, a fire-offering [**an offering**] of pleasing fragrance to [**which is accepted with favor before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:10)


וּדְאִשְׁתָּאַר מִן מִנְחָתָא לְאַהֲרֹן וְלִבְנוֹהִי קוֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין מִ**קֻּרְבָּנַיָּא** דַיְיָ:


Whatever remains of the meal-offering belongs to Aharon and his sons; [it is] holy of holies [most holy] of the fire-offering [**offerings**] of Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:11)


כָּל מִנְחָתָא דִּי תְקָרְבוּן **קֳדָם** יְיָ לָא תִתְעֲבֵד חֲמִיעַ אֲרֵי כָל חֲמִיר וְכָל דְּבַשׁ לָא תַסְּקוּן מִנֵּיהּ **קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


All meal-offerings that you bring to [**before**] Adonoy are not to be made leavened, because all sourdough and all honey you shall not burn from them a fire-offering to [**an offering before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:12)


קֻרְבַּן קַדְמַי תְּקָרְבוּן יָתְהוֹן **קֳדָם** יְיָ וּלְמַדְבְּחָא לָא יִתַּסְּקוּן **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא**:


You shall bring them as a first [fruit-]offering to [**before**] Adonoy but they shall not go up [be placed atop] the altar as a pleasing fragrance [**to be accepted with favor**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:13)


וְכָל קֻרְבַּן מִנְחָתָךְ בְּמִלְחָא תִמְלַח וְלָא תְבַטֵּל מְלַח קְיַם אֱלָהָךְ מֵעַל מִנְחָתָךְ עַל כָּל קֻרְבָּנָךְ תְּקָרֵב מִלְחָא:


You shall salt all your meal-offerings with salt and you shall not omit salt, the covenant of your God from [being placed] upon your meal-offerings. You shall bring salt on every one of your offerings.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:14)


וְאִם תְּקָרֵב מִנְחַת בִּכּוּרִין **קֳדָם** יְיָ אָבִיב קְלֵי בְנוּר פֵּירוֹכָן **רַכִּיכָן** תְּקָרֵיב יָת מִנְחַת בִּכּוּרָךְ:


When you bring a meal-offering of first grains to [**before**] Adonoy, of newly ripened crops, roasted over fire, ground [full and moist] [**soft**] kernels, you shall bring your first grain meal-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:15)


וְתִתֵּן עֲלַהּ מִשְׁחָא וּתְשַׁוֵּי עֲלַהּ לְבוֹנְתָּא מִנְחָתָא הִיא:


You shall put oil on it and place frankincense upon it; it is a meal-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/2:16)


וְיַסֵּק כַּהֲנָא יָת אִדְכַּרְתַּהּ מִפֵּרוּכַהּ וּמִמִּשְׁחַהּ עַל כָּל לְבוֹנְתָּהּ **קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם** יְיָ:


The *kohein* shall burn its memorial portion of its ground flour and of its oil and all of its frankincense; [it is] a fire-offering to [**an offering before**] Adonoy.



## Chapter 3

###### Onkelos Leviticus 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:1)


וְאִם נִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא** קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ אִם מִן תּוֹרֵי הוּא מְקָרֵב אִם דְּכַר אִם נוּקְבָא שְׁלִים יְקָרְבִינֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ:


If his offering is a peace [**sacred**]-offering; if his offering is of cattle [whether] male or female; he shall bring it unblemished before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:2)


וְיִסְמוֹךְ יְדֵיהּ עַל רֵישׁ קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ וְיִכְּסִנֵּיהּ בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיִזְרְקוּן בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא יָת דְּמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He shall lay his hand on the head of his offering and slaughter it at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting. The sons of Aharon, the *kohanim,* will sprinkle the blood all around the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:3)

He shall bring from the peace[**sacred**]-offering, as a fire-offering to [**an offering before**] Adonoy, the fat covering the innards, and all the fat on the innards.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:4)


וְיָת תַּרְתֵּין כָּלְיָן וְיָת תַּרְבָּא דִי עֲלֵיהֶן דִּי עַל גִּסְסַיָּא וְיָת חַצְרָא דְּעַל כַּבְדָא עַל כָּלְיֵתָא יַעֲדִּנָּהּ:


The two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver, he shall remove together with the kidneys.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:5)

The sons of Aharon shall burn it on the altar along with the burnt-offering that is on the logs which are on the fire; a fire-offering of pleasing fragrance to [**an offering which is accepted with favor before**] Adonoy.

If his offering is of sheep as a peace[**sacred**]-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, he shall bring an unblemished male or female.

If he is bringing a lamb as his offering, he shall bring it before Adonoy.

He shall lay his hand on the head of his offering and slaughter it at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting. The sons of Aharon, the *kohanim* will sprinkle its blood all around the altar.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/3:9)

The two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver he shall remove together with the kidneys.

The *kohein* shall burn it on the altar; it is the food of the fire-offering of [**offering before**] Adonoy.

If his offering is a goat, he shall bring it before Adonoy.

He shall bring from his offering as a fire-offering to [**an offering before**] Adonoy, the fat covering the innards, and all the fat on the innards,

the two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver he shall remove together with the kidneys.

He shall burn them on the altar, the food of the fire-offering for a pleasing fragrance [**offering to be accepted with favor**]; all fat [belongs] to [**before**] Adonoy.

As an everlasting statute for all your descendants in all of your dwelling places; you may not eat any fat, nor any blood.



## Chapter 4

###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:2)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימָר אֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יֵחוֹב בְּשָׁלוּ מִכָּל פִּקּוּדַיָּא דַיְיָ דִּי לָא **כַשְׁרִין** לְאִתְעֲבָדָא וְיַעְבֵּד מִן חַד מִנְּהוֹן:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying; if a person unwittingly transgresses any commandment of Adonoy which should [**is**] not [**fitting to**] be done and commits one of these sins.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:3)


אִם כַּהֲנָא **רַבָּא** יֵחוֹב לְחוֹבַת עַמָּא וִיקָרֵב עַל חוֹבְתֵיהּ דִּי חָב תּוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי שְׁלִים **קֳדָם** יְיָ לְחַטָּאתָא:


If the anointed *kohein* [**gadol**] should sin to bring guilt on the people, he shall bring for his sin which he committed a young, unblemished bullock to [**before**] Adonoy, as a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:4)


וְיַיְתִי יָת תּוֹרָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָקֳדָם יְיָ וְיִסְמוֹךְ יָת יְדֵיהּ עַל רֵישׁ תּוֹרָא וְיִכּוֹס יָת תּוֹרָא קָדֳם יְיָ:


He shall bring the bullock to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting before Adonoy. He shall lay his hand on the head of the bullock and slaughter the bullock before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:5)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא **רַבָּא** מִדְּמָא דְתוֹרָא וְיָעֵל יָתֵיהּ לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


The anointed *kohein* [**gadol**] shall take some of the bullocks blood and bring it inside the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:6)


וְיִטְבּוֹל כַּהֲנָא יָת אֶצְבְּעֵיהּ בִּדְמָא וְיַדִּי מִן דְּמָא שְׁבַע זִמְנִין קֳדָם יְיָ קֳדָם פָּרוּכְתָּא דְקוּדְשָׁא:


The *kohein* shall dip his finger into the blood and he shall sprinkle from the blood seven times, before Adonoy, in front of the Holy Curtain.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:7)


וְיִתֵּן כַּהֲנָא מִן דְּמָא עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבַּח דִקְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָא קֳדָם יְיָ דִּי בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיָת כָּל דְּמָא דְתוֹרָא יֵשׁוּד לִיסוֹדָא דְּמַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא דִּי בִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


The *kohein* shall put from the blood atop the corners of the spice-burning altar before Adonoy, which is inside the Tent of Meeting. He shall spill all the [remaining] bullocks blood into the base of the altar of the burnt-offering, which is at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.

the two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver, he shall remove together with the kidneys,

in the same manner as they were separated from the ox of the peace[**sacred**]-offering. The *kohein* shall burn them on the altar of the burnt-offering.

###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:34

וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא מִדְּמָא דְחַטָּאתָא בְּאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ וְיִתֵּן עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא דַעֲלָתָא וְיָת כָּל דְּמַהּ יֵשׁוֹד לִיסוֹדָא דְּמַדְבְּחָא:


The *kohein* shall take some blood from the sin-offering with his finger and put it atop the corners of the altar of the burnt-offering. He shall spill all its [remaining] blood into the base of the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 4:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/4:35)


וְיָת כָּל תַּרְבַּהּ יֶעְדֵּי כְּמָא דִי מִתַּעְדָּא תְרַב אִמְּרָא מִנִּכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא** וְיַסֵּק כַּהֲנָא יָתְהוֹן לְמַדְבְּחָא עַל **קֻרְבְּנַיָּא** דַיְיָ וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי כַּהֲנָא עַל חוֹבְתֵיהּ דִּי חָב וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לֵיהּ:


He shall remove all its fat in the same manner as was removed the fat of the lamb of the peace[**sacred**]-offering. The *kohein* shall burn them on the altar on the fires [**offerings**] of Adonoy. The *kohein* will atone for him, for his sin which he committed and he will be pardoned.



## Chapter 5

###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:1)


וֶאֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יֵחוֹב וְיִשְׁמַע קַל מוֹמֵי וְהוּא סָהִיד אוֹ חֲזָא אוֹ יְדָע אִם לָא יְחַוִּי וִיקַבֵּל חוֹבֵיהּ:


If a person sins, and heard an oath [demanding his testimony] [in a case where] he is a witness; [for] he either saw or knew [something relevant], if he does not testify, he will bear [the burden of] his iniquity.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:2)


אוֹ אֱנַשׁ דִּי יִקְרַב בְּכֹל מִדַּעַם מְסָאָב אוֹ בְנִבְלַת חַיְתָא מְסָאָבָא אוֹ בְּנִבְלַת בְּעִירָא מְסָאָבָא אוֹ בְּנִבְלַת רִחֲשָׁא מְסָאָב וִיהֵי מְכַסָּא מִנֵּיהּ וְהוּא מְסָאָב וְחָב:


Or, if a person touches anything impure either the carcass of an impure wild animal, or the carcass of an impure animal of pasture, or the carcass of an impure creeping animal; and this [impurity] was concealed from him, he is impure and incurs guilt.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:3)


אוֹ אֲרֵי יִקְרַב בְּסוֹאֲבַת אֱנָשָׁא לְכָל סוֹבְתֵיהּ דִּי יִסְתָּאַב בַּהּ וִיהֵי מְכַסָּא מִנֵּיהּ וְהוּא יָדַע וְחָב:


Or, if he touches the impurities of man whatever that impurity may be that causes impurity and this [impurity] was concealed from him; [when] he realizes [he is impure], he incurs guilt.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:4)


אוֹ אֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יְקַיֵּם לְפָרָשָׁא בְסִפְוָן לְאַבְאָשָׁא אוֹ לְאוֹטָבָא לְכֹל דִּי יְפָרֵשׁ אֱנָשָׁא בְּקִיּוּם וִיהֵי מְכַסָּא מִנֵּיהּ וְהוּא יָדַע וְחָב לַחֲדָא מֵאִלֵּין:


Or, if a person swears, orally expressing to do harm [that he will do something which is to his detriment] or to do good [that he will do something that is to his benefit]. Regarding all a person will expresses in an oath and [that oath] was concealed from himself, when he realizes [his sin] and he incurs guilt, for any one of these [aforementioned sins].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:5)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יֵחוֹב לַחֲדָא מֵאִלֵּין וִיוַדִּי דִּי חָב עֲלַהּ:


It shall be that when he incurs guilt for any of these [aforementioned sins] he shall confess that which he transgressed.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:6)


וְיַיְתִי יָת אַשְׁמֵיהּ **קֳדָם** יְיָ עַל חוֹבְתֵיהּ דִּי חָב נְקוּבְתָא מִן עָנָא אִמְּרָתָא אוֹ צְפִירַת עִזִּין לְחַטָאתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי כַּהֲנָא מֵחוֹבְתֵיהּ:


He shall bring his guilt-offering to [**before**] Adonoy for his sin that he committed a female of the sheep, a lamb or a she-goat as a sin-offering. The *kohein* will atone for him, for his sin.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:7)


וְאִם לָא תִמְטֵי יְדֵיהּ כְּמִסַּת שֵׂיתָא וְיַיְתִי יָת חוֹבְתֵיהּ דִּי חָב תַּרְתֵּין שַׁפְנִינִין אוֹ תְרֵין בְּנֵי יוֹנָה **קֳדָם** יְיָ חַד לְחַטָּאתָא וְחַד לַעֲלָתָא:


If his means are not sufficient for [the acquisition of] a sheep, then he shall bring for his guilt-offering,—[he] who has sinned,—two turtledoves or two young pigeons to [**before**] Adonoy one for a sin-offering and one for a burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:8)


וְיַיְתִי יָתְהוֹן לְוָת כַּהֲנָא וִיקָרֵב יָת דִּי לְחַטָּאתָא קַדְמֵיתָא וְיִמְלוֹק יָת רֵישֵׁיהּ מִלָּקֳבֵל קְדָלֵיהּ וְלָא יַפְרֵשׁ:


He shall bring them to the *kohein* and he shall bring that which is for a sin-offering first. He [the *kohein*] shall shear its head [with his fingernail] opposite of its neck, but not separate [the head] completely.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:9)


וְיַדִּי מִדְּמָא דְחַטָּאתָא עַל כֹּתֶל מַדְבְּחָא וּדְאִשְׁתָּאַר בִּדְמָא יִתִּמְצֵי לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא חַטָּאתָא הוּא:


He shall sprinkle some blood of the sin-offering on to the wall of the altar. And the remaining blood shall be squeezed into the base of the altar; it is a sin-offering.

He shall do (offer) the second [turtledove pigeon] as a burnt-offering, as prescribed. The *kohein* will atone from him, for his sin that he committed and he will be pardoned.

וְאִם לָא תַדְבֵּק יְדֵיהּ לְתַּרְתֵּין שַׁפְנִינִין אוֹ לִתְרֵין בְּנֵי יוֹנָה וְיַיְתִי יָת קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ דִּי חָב חַד מִן עַסְרָא **בִתְלַת סְאִין** סֻלְתָּא לְחַטָּאתָא לָא יְשַׁוִּי עֲלַהּ מִשְׁחָא וְלָא יִתֵּן עֲלַהּ לְבֻנְתָּא אֲרֵי חַטָּאתָא הִיא:


If his means are not sufficient for the acquisition of two turtledoves or two young pigeons, he shall bring as his offering,—[he] who has sinned,—one tenth of an epha [**which is three se’in**] of fine flour as a sin-offering. If a person wrongfully deviates [**lies**] and unwittingly sins [by using the] sacred property of Adonoy, he shall bring his guilt-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, an unblemished ram, from the sheep, based on the valuation of holy silver shekalim [**selaim**], for a guilt-offering.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:16)

That which he sinned [making use] of the sacred property, he shall pay for and add an additional fifth to that [amount] and give it to the *kohein.* The *kohein* will atone for him with the guilt-offering ram and he will be pardoned.

If a person sins and did [transgress] one of the commandments of Adonoy that should [**is**] not [**fitting to**] be done, [however,] he is uncertain [that he sinned], and he [nevertheless] incurs guilt and will bear [the burden of] his iniquity.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:18)

He shall bring an unblemished ram, from the sheep, based on the valuation as a guilt-offering, to the *kohein.* The *kohein* will atone for him, for the error which he did unintentionally, and he did not know, and he will be pardoned.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:21)


אֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יֵחוֹב וִישַׁקַּר **שְׁקַר קֳדָם** יְיָ וִיכַדֵב בְּחַבְרֵיהּ בּפִקְּדוֹנָא אוֹ בְשֻׁתָּפוּת יְדָא אוֹ בִגְזֵלָא אוֹ אַנִּיס יָת חַבְרֵיהּ:


If a person sins and wrongfully deviates [**lies**] from [**before**] Adonoy and denies to his friend the possession of a keepsake placed in his trust or, a deposit [regarding money that was loaned or in partnership,] or theft [claiming that something was stolen,] or, oppressed [he withheld wages from] his friend.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:22)

Or, if he found a lost item and denies it and swears falsely in any of all these [aforementioned instances], whatever someone will do to sin in these instances.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:23)


וִיהֵי אֲרֵי יֶחֱטֵי וְיֵחוֹב וְיָתֵב יָת גְּזֵלָא דִּי גְזַל אוֹ יָת עֻשְׁקָא דִּי עֲשַׁק אוֹ יָת פִּקְּדוֹנָא דִּי אִתְפָּקַּד לְוָתֵיהּ אוֹ יָת אֲבֵדְתָּא דִּי אַשְׁכַּח:


It shall be that when he [realizes his] sin and incurs guilt, he shall return the stolen property that he stole, or, the wages that he withheld or, whatever keepsake that was placed in his trust or, the lost item which he found.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:24)


אוֹ מִכֹּל דִּי יִשְׁתְּבַע עֲלוֹהִי לְשִׁקְרָא וִישַׁלֵּם יָתֵיהּ בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ וְחֻמְשֵׁיהּ יוֹסֵף עֲלוֹהִי לְדִי הוּא דִילֵיהּ יִתְּנִנֵּיהּ בְּיוֹמָא דְחוֹבְתֵיהּ:


Or, anything about which he will swear falsely. He shall pay its price and add an additional fifth to that [amount] to whomever that object belongs he shall give it on the day of his guilt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:25)


וְיָת אַשְׁמַהּ יַיְתִי לָקֳדָם יְיָ דְּכַר שְׁלִים מִן עָנָא בְּפֻרְסָנֵיהּ לַאֲשָׁמָא לְוָת כַּהֲנָא:


He shall bring his guilt-offering to [**before**] Adonoy an unblemished ram, from the sheep based on the valuation, as a guilt-offering to the *kohein.*


###### Onkelos Leviticus 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/5:26)


וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי כַהֲנָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לֵיהּ עַל חֲדָא מִכֹּל דִּי יַעְבֵּד לְמֵיחַב בַּהּ:


The *kohein* will atone for him before Adonoy and he will be pardoned for anything that he will do through which guilt incurs.

Command Aharon and his sons, saying; this is the law of the burnt-offering. It is the burnt-offering [which remains] on its pyre on the altar all night, until the morning, and the fire of the altar will be kept burning on it.

The *kohein* shall dress in his linen garment and he shall wear linen pants on his [body] skin; and he shall separate the ashes when the fire consumes the burnt-offering on the altar and place it next to the altar.

This is the law of the meal-offering. The sons of Aharon bring it before Adonoy, to the front of the altar.

Speak to Aharon and his sons, saying: this is the law of the sin-offering. In the place where the burnt-offering is slaughtered, the sin-offering shall be slaughtered, before Adonoy; it is holy of holies [most holy].

The *kohein* who offers it as a sin-offering [**atones with its blood**], shall eat it. It shall be eaten in a sacred place, in the Courtyard of the Tent of Meeting.

Whatever shall touch its flesh shall become holy and when of its blood is sprinkled on the garment, whatever it is sprinkled upon, you shall wash in a sacred place.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/6:21)


וּמָן דַּחֲסַף דִּי תִתְבַּשֵּׁל בֵּיהּ יִתָּבָר וְאִם בְּמָנָא דִּנְחָשָׁא תִּתְבַּשֵּׁל וְיִתְמְרֵק וְיִשְׁתַּטֵּף בְּמַיָּא:


An earthenware vessel in which it was cooked, must be broken. If it was cooked in a copper vessel, it must be scoured and rinsed with water.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/6:22)

Every male among the *kohanim* may eat it; it is holy of holies [=most holy].

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/6:23)


וְכָל חַטָּאתָא דִּי יִתָּעַל מִדְּמַהּ לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְכַפָּרָא בְקוּדְשָׁא לָא תִתְאֲכֵל בְּנוּרָא תִּתּוֹקָד:


Every sin-offering whose blood is to be brought inside the Tent of Meeting to bring about atonement inside the Sanctuary may not be eaten; it must be burned in fire.



## Chapter 7

###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:1)

This is the law of the guilt-offering; it is holy of holies [=most holy].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:2)


בְאַתְרָא דִּי יִכְּסוּן יָת עֲלָתָא יִכְּסוּן יָת אֲשָׁמָא וְיָת דְּמֵיהּ יִזְרַק עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


In the place where they will slaughter the burnt-offering they shall slaughter the guilt-offering, and they shall sprinkle its blood all around the altar.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:3)


וְיָת כָּל תַּרְבֵּיהּ **יַפְרֵשׁ** מִנֵּיהּ יָת אֲלִיתָא וְיָת תַּרְבָּא דְּחָפֵי יָת גַּוָּא:


He shall bring [**separate**] all its fat from it; the [fat] tail, the fat covering the innards,


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:4)


וְיָת תַּרְתֵּין כָּלְיָן וְיָת תַּרְבָּא דִּי עֲלֵיהֶן דִּי עַל גִּסְסַיָּא וְיָת חִצְרָא דְּעַל כַּבְדָּא עַל כָּלְיֵתָא יֶעְדִּנַּהּ:


the two kidneys and the fat on them that are on the flanks. The lobe on the liver, he shall remove together with the kidneys.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:5)


וְיַסֵּק יָתְהוֹן כַּהֲנָא לְמַדְבְּחָא **קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם** יְיָ אֲשָׁמָא הוּא:


The *kohein* shall burn them on the altar, a fire-offering to [**an offering before**] Adonoy; it is a guilt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:6)


כָּל דְּכוּרָא בְכַהֲנַיָּא יֵיכְלִנֵּיהּ בַּאֲתַר קַדִּישׁ יִתְאֲכָל קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הוּא:


All males among the *kohanim* may eat it. It must be eaten in a sacred place; it is most holy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:7)


כְּחַטָּאתָא כַּאֲשָׁמָא אוֹרַיְתָא חֲדָא לְהוֹן כַּהֲנָא דִּי יְכַפֵּר בֵּיהּ דִּילֵיהּ יְהֵא:


As the sin-offering is, so is the guilt-offering; one law applies to them both; the *kohein* who will bring about atonement with it, it shall belong to him.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:8)

The *kohein* who brings a burnt-offering of an individual, the skin of the burnt-offering that he brought, shall belong to the *kohein,* it shall belong to him.

וְכָל מִנְחָתָא דִּי תִתְאֲפֵי בְּתַנּוּרָא וְכָל דְּתִתְעֲבֵד **בְּרִדְתָּא** וְעַל מַסְרֵיתָא לְכַהֲנָא דִּמְקָרֵב יָתַהּ דִּילֵיהּ תְּהֵא:


Every meal-offering that shall be baked in an oven and every one made in a deep pot [**which appears as if it has been ploughed out**] or frying pan, [belong] to the *kohein* who offers it; it shall belong to him.

Every meal-offering mixed with oil or dry [**not mixed**], shall belong to all the sons of Aharon, each equally.

This is the law of the peace[**sacred**]-offering that one will bring to [**before**] Adonoy.

If he brings it as thanksgiving offering he shall bring along with his thanksgiving offering *matzoh* [unleavened] loaves mixed with oil, *matzoh* [unleavened] wafers anointed with oil and loaves of saturated fine flour mixed with oil.

The flesh of his thanksgiving peace[**sacred**]-offering on the day of its sacrifice it may be eaten. He shall not leave over any of it until morning.

Whatever flesh is left over from the offering shall be burned in fire, on the third day.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:18

וְאִם אִתְאֲכָלָא יִתְאֲכֵל מִבְּסַר נִכְסַת **קוּדְשׁוֹהִי** בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה לָא יְהֵא לְרַעֲוָא דִּמְקָרֵב יָתֵיהּ לָא יִתְחֲשֵׁב לֵיהּ **מְרָחָק** יְהֵא וֶאֱנַשׁ דְּתֵיכוּל מִנֵּיהּ חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּל:


If it will be eaten, of the flesh of the peace[**sacred**]-offering on the third day, it will not be favorably accepted. [As to] whoever brings it, it will not be credited to him; it is an abomination [**rejected**] and anyone who eats of it shall bear [the burden of] its iniquity.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:19)

The flesh [of [**the sacred**] offerings] that will touch any unclean [thing] may not be eaten; it shall be burned in fire. [As to] the [pure] [**sacred**] flesh, every [ritually] pure person [**fitting for the sacred**] may eat [the] [**sacred**] meat.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:20

וֶאֱנַשׁ דִּי תֵיכוּל בִּסְרָא מִנִּכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא דִּי קֳדָם** יְיָ וְסוֹבְתֵיהּ עֲלוֹהִי וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי אֱנָשָׁא הַהִיא מֵעַמַּהּ:


The person who eats of the flesh of the peace[**sacred**]-offering belonging to [**which is before**] Adonoy while his uncleaness is yet on him, that persons soul shall be cut off from its people.

A person who touches anything unclean, be it the impurities of man or an unclean animal, or any unclean creeping creature and then eats some flesh of the peace[**sacred**]-offering belonging to [**which is before**] Adonoy, that persons soul shall be cut off from its people.

The fat of an improperly slaughtered animal and the fat of a fatally wounded animal may be used for any purpose, but you are surely not permitted to eat it.

You shall not eat any blood, in all your dwelling places, be it of fowl or of beast.

Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying: Whoever shall bring the sacrifice of his peace[**sacred**]-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, he shall bring his offering to [**before**] Adonoy from his peace[**sacred**]-offering.

אֲרֵי יָת חֶדְיָא דַ**אֲרָמוּתָא** וְיָת שׁוֹקָא דְ**אַפְרָשׁוּתָא** נְסֵבִית מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִנִּכְסַת **קוּדְשֵׁיהוֹן** וִיהָבִית יָתְהוֹן לְאַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא וְלִבְנוֹהִי לִקְיָם עָלָם מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Because the breast wave[**uplifted**]-offering and the thigh [that is] the uplifted [**separated**] portion I have taken from Bnei Yisroel. from their peace[**sacred**]-offerings and I have given them to Aharon the *kohein* and to his sons, as an everlasting statute, from Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 7:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:35)

This is [for] the anointing [**inauguration to greatness**] of Aharon and [for] the anointing [**inauguration to greatness**] of his sons from the fire-offering [**offerings**] of Adonoy, on the day He brought them to serve before Adonoy,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:36)

that Adonoy commanded to give to them, on the day He anointed [**inaugurated**] them [**to greatness**], from Bnei Yisroel, an everlasting statute for all their descendants.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:37)

This is the law for the burnt-offering, the meal-offering, the sin-offering and the guilt-offering; for the installation-sacrifices [**sacrifices**] and for the peace[**sacred**]-offering,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/7:38)


דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה בְּטוּרָא דְסִינָי בְּיוֹמָא דִפַקְּדֵיהּ יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְקָרָבָא יָת קֻרְבָּנְהוֹן **קֳדָם** יְיָ בְּמַדְבְּרָא דְסִינָי:


that Adonoy commanded Moshe on Mount Sinai, on the day that he commanded Bnei Yisroel to bring their offerings to [**before**] Adonoy in the desert of Sinai.



## Chapter 8

###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:1

And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, to say:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:2)


**קָרֵב** יָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ וְיָת לְבוּשַׁיָּא וְיָת מִשְׁחָא דִ**רְבוּתָא** וְיָת תּוֹרָא דְחַטָּאתָא וְיָת תְּרֵין דִּכְרִין וְיָת סַלָּא דְפַטִּירַיָּא:


Take [**Bring**] Aharon [**near**], along with his sons, the garments, the anointing oil [**of inaugurating to greatness**], the bull for the sin-offering, the two rams and a basket of *matzos*.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:3)


וְיָת כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא כְּנֵשׁ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Assemble the entire congrega-tion at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:4

וַעֲבַד משֶׁה כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָתֵיהּ וְאִתְכְּנִישַׁת כְּנִשְׁתָּא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


Moshe did that which Adonoy commanded him and the congregation was assembled at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.

וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לִכְנִשְׁתָּא דֵּין פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ לְמֶעְבָּד:


Moshe said to the congregation, This is the edict that Adonoy commanded to do.

וְקָרִיב משֶׁה יָת אַהֲרֹן וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי וְאַסְחֵי יָתְהוֹן בְּמַיָּא:


Moshe brought Aharon and his sons near and bathed them in water.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:7)


וִיהַב עֲלוֹהִי יָת כִּתּוּנָא וְזָרֵז יָתֵיהּ בְּהֶמְיָנָא וְאַלְבֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ יָת מְעִילָא וִיהַב עֲלוֹהִי יָת אֵפוֹדָא וְזָרֵז יָתֵיהּ בְּהֶמְיַן אֵפוֹדָא וְאַתְקֵן לֵיהּ בֵּיהּ:


He placed the tunic on him, girded him with the sash, dressed him in the robe, placed the *ephod* on him girded him with the *ephod*-sash and tightened it for him.

He put the breastplate on him and he placed in the breastplate the *urim* and the *tumim.*

Moshe took the anointing-oil [**of inaugurating to greatness**] and anointed [**inaugurated**] the Tabernacle [**to greatness**] and everything inside it and sanctified them.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:13)


וְקָרִיב משֶׁה יָת בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן וְאַלְבֵּשִׁנּוּן כִּתּוּנִין וְזָרֵז יָתְהוֹן הֶמְיָנִין וְאַתְקֵן לְהוֹן כּוֹבָעִין כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:15)


וּנְכֵס וּנְסִיב משֶׁה יָת דְּמָא וִיהַב עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר בְּאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ וְדַכִּי יָת מַדְבְּחָא וְיָת דְּמָא אֲרִיק לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא וְקַדְּשֵׁיהּ לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹהִי:


He slaughtered it; and Moshe took the blood and put it atop the corners of the altar all around with his finger and he purified the altar. He poured the blood into the base of the altar and sanctified it, to atone upon it.

He took all the fat on the innards, the lobe of the liver, the two kidneys and their fats and Moshe burned [them] on the altar.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:17)

He brought the burnt-offering ram and Aharon and his sons laid their hands on the rams head.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:19)

He slaughtered it; and Moshe sprinkled the blood all around the altar.

וְיָת דִּכְרָא פַּלֵּיג לְאֶבְרוֹהִי וְאַסֵּק משֶׁה יָת רֵישָׁא וְיָת אֵבָרַיָּא וְיָת תַּרְבָּא:


He severed the rams limbs; and Moshe burned the head and the severed limbs and the fat.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:21)


וְיָת גַּוָּא וְיָת כְּרָעַיָּא חַלִּיל בְּמַיָּא וְאַסֵּק משֶׁה יָת כָּל דִּכְרָא לְמַדְבְּחָא עֲלָתָא הוּא **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֻרְבָּנָא הוּא קֳדָם** יְיָ כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


He washed the innards and feet in water; and Moshe burned the entire ram on the altar. It is a burnt-offering; for a pleasing fragrance [**to be accepted with favor**], a fire-offering to [**an offering before**] Adonoy, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:22)

He brought the second ram, the ram for the installation-offering [**offerings**]; and Aharon and his sons laid their hands on the rams head.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:23)


וּנְכֵס וּנְסִיב משֶׁה מִן דְּמָא וִיהַב עַל **רוּם אוּדְנָא** דְאַהֲרֹן דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן יְדֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן רַגְלֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא:


He slaughtered it; and Moshe took some of its blood and he put it on [**the upper part of**] Aharons right earlobe [**ear**], on the thumb of his right hand and the big toe of his right foot.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:24)


וְקָרִיב יָת בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן וִיהַב משֶׁה מִן דְּמָא עַל **רוּם אוּדְנֵהוֹן** דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן יְדֵיהוֹן דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן רַגְלֵהוֹן דְּיָמִּינָא וּזְרַק משֶׁה יָת דְּמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He brought the sons of Aharon; and Moshe put some blood on [**the upper part of**] their right earlobes [**ears**] and on the thumbs of their right hands and on the big toes of their right feet. Moshe sprinkled the blood all around the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:25)


וּנְסִיב יָת תַּרְבָּא וְיָת אֲלִיתָא וְיָת כָּל תַּרְבָּא דִּי עַל גַּוָּא וְיָת חֲצַר כַּבְדָּא וְיָת תַּרְתֵּין כָּלְיָן וְיָת תַּרְבְּהֶן וְיָת שׁוֹקָא דְיַמִּינָא:


He took the fat and the fat tail, all the fat on the innards, the lobe of the liver, the two kidneys and their fats and the right thigh.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:26)


וּמִסַּלָּא דְפַטִּירַיָּא דִּי קֳדָם יְיָ נְסִיב גְּרִצְתָּא פַטִּירָא חֲדָא וּגְרִצְתָּא דִלְחֵם מְשַׁח חֲדָא וְאֶסְפּוֹג חָד וְשַׁוִּי עַל תַּרְבַּיָּא וְעַל שׁוֹקָא דְיַמִּינָא:


From the basket of *matzos* which [was] before Adonoy he took one *matzoh* loaf, one oil-bread and one wafer and he placed them on the fats and on the right thigh.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:27)


וִיהַב יָת כֹּלָּא עַל יְדֵי אַהֲרֹן וְעַל יְדֵי בְנוֹהִי **וַאֲרֵם** יָתְהוֹן **אֲרָמָא** קֳדָם יְיָ:


He put them all on Aharons palms and on the palms of his sons and he waved [**lifted**] them [**up**] as a wave-offering [**an uplifted-offering**] before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:28)


וּנְסִיב משֶׁה יָתְהוֹן מֵעַל יְדֵיהוֹן וְאַסֵּק לְמַדְבְּחָא עַל עֲלָתָא **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא אִנּוּן לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא קֻרְבָּנָא** הוּא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Moshe took them from their palms and he burned them on the altar in addition to the burnt-offering; they are installation-offerings for a pleasing fragrance [**offerings to be accepted with favor**], it is a fire-offering to [**an offering before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:29)


וּנְסִיב משֶׁה יָת חֶדְיָא **וַאֲרִימֵיהּ אֲרָמָא** קֳדָם יְיָ מִדְּכַר **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** לְמשֶׁה הֲוָה לָחֳלָק כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


Moshe took the breast and waved [**lifted**] it [**up**] [as] a wave-offering [**an uplifted-offering**] before Adonoy. From the ram for the installation-offering [**offerings**]—that was the portion of Moshe—just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:30)


וּנְסִיב משֶׁה מִמִּשְׁחָא **דִרְבוּתָא** וּמִן דְּמָא דִּי עַל מַדְבְּחָא וְאַדִּי עַל אַהֲרֹן עַל לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְעַל בְּנוֹהִי וְעַל לְבוּשֵׁי בְנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ וְקַדִּישׁ יָת אַהֲרֹן יָת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי וְיָת לְבוּשֵׁי בְנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ:


Moshe took some anointing-oil [**which inaugurates to greatness**] and some blood that was on the altar and sprinkled it on Aharon, on his garments, and on his sons and on his sons garments with him. He sanctified Aharon and his garments and his sons and his sons garments with him.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:31)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרֹן וְלִבְנוֹהִי בַּשִּׁילוּ יָת בִּסְרָא בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְתַמָּן תֵּיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ וְיָת לַחְמָא דִּי בְּסַל **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** כְּמָא דִי פַקֵּדִית לְמֵימַר אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי יֵיכְלֻנֵּיהּ:


Moshe said to Aharon and to his sons Cook the flesh at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting and you shall eat it there [with] the bread that is in the installation basket [**of sacrifices**] just as I commanded, saying; Aharon and his sons shall eat them.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:32)


וּדְאִשְׁתָּאַר בְּבִשְׂרָא וּבְלַחְמָא בְּנוּרָא תּוֹקְדוּן:


Whatever flesh and bread is left over, you shall burn them in fire.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:33)


וּמִתְּרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָא תִפְּקוּן שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין עַד יוֹם מִשְׁלַם יוֹמֵי **קֻרְבָּנֵיכוֹן** אֲרֵי שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין **יְקָרֵב יָת קֻרְבָּנְכוֹן**:


From the entrance of the Tent of Meeting you shall not go out for seven days until the time that your days of installation [**sacrifices**] are complete, because for seven days your hands [**sacrifices**] will be filled [**offered**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:34)


כְּמָא דִי עֲבַד בְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין פַּקִּיד יְיָ לְמֶעְבַּד לְכַפָּרָא עֲלֵיכוֹן:


Just as you have done on this day, Adonoy commanded him to do, to bring about atonement.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 8:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:35)


וּבִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא תֵּיתְבוּן יְמָם וְלֵילֵי שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין וְתִטְּרוּן יָת מַטְּרַת **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וְלָא תְמוּתוּן אֲרֵי כֵן אִתְפַּקָּדִית:


At the entrance of the Tent of Meeting you shall sit day and night for seven days and keep the charge of [**the Word of**] Adonoy and you will not die, because I have been thus commanded.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/8:36)


וַעֲבַד אַהֲרֹן וּבְנוֹהִי יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה:


Aharon and his sons fulfilled all the edicts that Adonoy commanded through Moshe.



## Chapter 9

###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:1)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה קְרָא משֶׁה לְאַהֲרֹן וְלִבְנוֹהִי וּלְסָבֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


And it was on the eight day [that] Moshe called to Aharon and to his sons and to the elders of Israel.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:2)


וַאֲמַר לְאַהֲרֹן סַב לָךְ עֶגַל בַּר תּוֹרֵי לְחַטָּאתָא וּדְכַר לַעֲלָתָא שַׁלְמִין וְקָרֵב קֳדָם יְיָ:


He said to Aharon, Take, for yourself, a young calf, for a sin-offering and a ram—for a burnt-offering—unblemished ones, and bring them before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:3)


וְעִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּמַלֵּל לְמֵימָר סִיבוּ צְפִיר בַּר עִזִּין לְחַטָּאתָא וְעֵגָל וְאִמָּר בְּנֵי שְׁנָא שַׁלְמִין לַעֲלָתָא:


To Bnei Yisroel you shall speak, saying, take a he-goat, for a sin-offering and a calf and a lamb, both one year old—unblemished—for a burnt-offering,


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:4)


וְתוֹר וּדְכָר לְנִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא** לְדַבָּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִנְחָתָא דְּפִילָא בִמְשָׁח אֲרֵי יוֹמָא דֵין **יְקָרָא** דַיְיָ **מִתְגְּלִי** לְכוֹן:


an ox and a ram for a peace[**sacred**]-offering, to sacrifice before Adonoy and a meal-offering mixed with oil, for today, [**the Glory of**] Adonoy will appear [**become revealed**] to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:5)


וּנְסִיבוּ יָת דִּי פַקִּיד משֶׁה לָקֳדָם מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּקְרִיבוּ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא וְקָמוּ קֳדָם יְיָ:


They took that which Moshe commanded, before the Tent of Meeting; and the entire congregation approached and they stood before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:6)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה דֵּין פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ תַּעְבְּדוּן **וְיִתְגְּלִי** לְכוֹן יְקָרָא דַיְיָ:


Moshe said: This is what Adonoy commanded you to do: and the glory of Adonoy will appear [**become revealed**] to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:7)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרֹן קְרַב לְמַדְבְּחָא וְעִבֵד יָת חַטָּאתָךְ וְיָת עֲלָתָךְ וְכַפַּר עֲלָךְ וְעַל עַמָּא וְעִבֵד יָת קֻרְבַּן עַמָּא וְכַפַּר עֲלֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ:


Moshe said to Aharon: Approach the altar and perform [the services of] your sin-offering and your burnt-offering and atone on your [own] behalf and on behalf of the people; and perform [the service of] the peoples offering and atone on their behalf, just as Adonoy commanded.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:8)


וּקְרֵב אַהֲרֹן לְמַדְבְּחָא וּנְכֵס יָת עֶגְלָא דְחַטָּאתָא דִּי לֵיהּ:


Aharon approached the altar and slaughtered the calf that he had for a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:9)


וְקָרִיבוּ בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן יָת דְּמָא לֵיהּ וּטְבַל אֶצְבְּעֵיהּ בִּדְמָא וִיהַב עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא וְיָת דְּמָא אֲרִיק לִיסוֹדָא דְמַדְבְּחָא:


Aharons sons brought the blood to him and he dipped his finger into the blood and put it atop the corners of the altar and he poured the [remaining] blood into the base of the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:10)


וְיָת תַּרְבָּא וְיָת כָּלְיְתָא וְיָת חִצְרָא מִן כַּבְדָּא מִן חַטָּאתָא אַסֵּק לְמַדְבְּחָא כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


The fat, kidneys and the lobe of the liver from the sin-offering, he burned on the altar, just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:11)


וְיָת בִּשְׂרָא וְיָת מַשְׁכָּא אוֹקִיד בְּנוּרָא מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


The flesh and the skin, he burned in a fire beyond the encampment.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:12)


וּנְכֵס יָת עֲלָתָא וְאַמְטִיאוּ בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן לֵיהּ יָת דְּמָא וּזְרָקֵיהּ עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He slaughtered the burnt-offering; and Aharons sons presented the blood to him, and he sprinkled it all around the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:13)


וְיָת עֲלָתָא אַמְטִיאוּ לֵיהּ לְאֵבָרָהָא וְיָת רֵישָׁא וְאַסֵּק עַל מַדְבְּחָא:


They presented the burnt-offering to him with its limbs severed and [with] the head, and he burned them on the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:14)


וְחַלִּיל יָת גַּוָּא וְיָת כְּרָעַיָּא וְאַסֵּק עַל עֲלָתָא לְמַדְבְּחָא:


He washed the innards and the feet and burned them on the burnt-offering, on the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:15)


וְקָרֵב יָת קֻרְבַּן עַמָּא וּנְסִיב יָת צְפִירָא דְחַטָּאתָא דִּי לְעַמָּא וְנָכְסֵיהּ **וְכַפֵּר בִּדְמֵיהּ** כְּקַדְמָאָה:


He brought the peoples offering; he took the peoples sin-offering-goat, and he slaughtered it and offered it as a sin-offering [**atoned with its blood**], in the same manner as the first one.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:16)


וְקָרֵב יָת עֲלָתָא וְעָבְדַהּ כִּדְחָזֵי:


He brought the burnt-offering and he did it according to the rule.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:17)


וְקָרֵב יָת מִנְחָתָא וּמְלָא יְדֵיהּ מִנַּהּ וְאַסֵּק עַל מַדְבְּחָא בַּר מֵעֲלַת צַפְרָא:


He brought the meal-offering; he filled his palm from it and burned it on the altar: aside from the burnt-offering [that was brought] in the morning.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:18)


וּנְכֵס יָת תּוֹרָא וְיָת דִּכְרָא נִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא** דִּי לְעַמָּא וְאַמְטִיאוּ בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן יָת דְּמָא לֵיהּ וּזְרָקֵיהּ עַל מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He slaughtered the ox and the ram, the peace[**sacred**]-offerings of the people; and Aharons sons presented the blood to him and he sprinkled it all around the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:19)


וְיָת תַּרְבַּיָּא מִן תּוֹרָא וּמִן דִּכְרָא אֲלִיתָא וְחָפֵי גַוָּא וְכָלְיְתָא וַחֲצַר כַּבְדָּא:


The fats of the ox and of the ram; the fat-tail, the [fat] covering [the innards], the kidneys and the lobe of the liver.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:20)


וְשַׁוִּיאוּ יָת תַּרְבַּיָּא עַל חֶדְוָתָא וְאַסֵּק תַּרְבַּיָּא לְמַדְבְּחָא:


They put the fats on top of the breasts and he burned the fats on the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:21)


וְיָת חֶדְוָתָא וְיָת שׁוֹקָא דְיַמִּינָא **אֲרֵם** אַהֲרֹן **אֲרָמָא** קֳדָם יְיָ כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד משֶׁה:


The breasts and the right thigh, Aharon waved [**lifted up**] [as] a wave-offering [**an uplifted-offering**] before Adonoy, just as Moshe commanded.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:22)


וַאֲרֵם אַהֲרֹן יָת יְדוֹהִי עַל עַמָּא וּבָרֵכִנּוּן וּנְחַת מִלְּמֶעְבַּד חַטָּאתָא וַעֲלָתָא וְנִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא**:


Aharon raised his hands toward the people and he blessed them. He descended [from the altar where he] had offered the sin-offering, the burnt-offering and the peace[**sacred**]-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:23)


וְעַל משֶׁה וְאַהֲרֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וּנְפָקוּ וּבָרִיכוּ יָת עַמָּא **וְאִתְגְּלִי** יְקָרָא דַיְיָ לְכָל עַמָּא:


And Moshe and Aharon went [in]to the Tent of Meeting and [then] they went outside and blessed the people, and the glory of Adonoy appeared [**became revealed**] to the entire people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/9:24)


וּנְפָקַת אֶשָּׁתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲכָלַת עַל מַדְבְּחָא יָת עֲלָתָא וְיָת תַּרְבַּיָּא וַחֲזָא כָּל עַמָּא וְשַׁבָּחוּ וּנְפָלוּ עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן:


A fire came forth from before Adonoy and consumed what was on the altar; the burnt-offering and the fats. All the people saw and they raised their voices in praise, and they fell on their faces.



## Chapter 10

###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:1)


וּנְסִיבוּ בְנֵי אַהֲרֹן נָדָב וַאֲבִיהוּא גְּבַר מַחְתִּיתֵיהּ וִיהָבוּ בְהוֹן אֶשָּׁתָא וְשַׁוִּיאוּ עֲלַהּ קְטוֹרֶת וְקָרִיבוּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֶשָּׁתָא נוּכְרֵיתָא דִּי לָא פַּקִּיד יָתְהוֹן:


Nodov and Avihu, Aharons sons, took, each of them his fire-pan, placed fire on it and then placed incense upon it and they brought before Adonoy a strange fire, which He had not commanded them.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:2)


וּנְפָקַת אֶשָּׁתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וַאֲכָלַת יָתְהוֹן וּמִיתוּ קֳדָם יְיָ:


A fire came forth from before Adonoy and consumed them, and they died in the presence of Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:3)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרֹן הוּא דִי מַלִּיל יְיָ לְמֵימַר בְּקָרִיבַי אִתְקַדַּשׁ וְעַל אַפֵּי כָל עַמָּא אִתְיַקָּר וּשְׁתִיק אַהֲרֹן:


Moshe said to Aharon It is as Adonoy spoke, saying: through those that are near me I shall be sanctified, and in the presence of the entire people I will be glorified, and Aharon was silent.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:4)


וּקְרָא משֶׁה לְמִישָׁאֵל וּלְאֶלְצָפָן בְּנֵי עֻזִּיאֵל **אַח אֲבוּהִי** דְאַהֲרֹן וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן קְרִיבוּ טוּלוּ יָת אֲחוּכוֹן מִן קֳדָם אַפֵּי קוּדְשָׁא לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


Moshe called to Mishael and to Elzafan the sons of Uziel, [**the brother of the father of**] Aharon’s uncle, and said to them; Come closer and carry your brothers from the presence of the Sanctuary, to beyond the encampment.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:5)


וּקְרִיבוּ וּנְטָלֻנּוּן בְּכִתֻּנֵּיהוֹן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא כְּמָא דִי מַלִּיל משֶׁה:


They came closer and they carried them in their tunics to beyond the encampment, just as Moshe spoke.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:6)


וַאֲמַר משֶׁה לְאַהֲרֹן וּלְאֶלְעָזָר וּלְאִיתָמָר בְּנוֹהִי רֵישֵׁיכוֹן לָא תְרַבּוּן פֵּרוּעַ וּלְבוּשֵׁיכוֹן לָא תְבַזְּעוּן וְלָא תְמוּתוּן וְעַל כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא יְהֵי רוּגְזָא וַאֲחֵיכוֹן כָּל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל יִבְכּוּן יָת יְקִדְתָּא דִּי אוֹקִיד יְיָ:


Moshe said to Aharon and to Elozor and Isomor, his sons: Do not let your hair grow long and do not tear your garments so that you will not die and bring [Divine] anger on the entire congregation; your brothers, the entire House of Yisroel shall weep over the conflagration that Adonoy set ablaze.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:7)


וּמִתְּרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָא תִפְּקוּן דִּילְמָא תְמוּתוּן אֲרֵי מְשַׁח **רְבוּתָא** דַיְיָ עֲלֵיכוֹן וַעֲבָדוּ כְּפִתְגָּמָא דְמשֶׁה:


From the entrance of the Tent of Meeting you shall not go forth lest you die; for the anointing oil of [**the inauguration to greatness of**] Adonoy is on you and they did as Moshe had said.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:8)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ לְאַהֲרֹן לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Aharon, saying,


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:9)


חֲמַר וּמְרַוֵּי לָא תִשְׁתֵּי אַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ עִמָּךְ בְּמֵעַלְכוֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְלָא תְמוּתוּן קְיַם עָלָם לְדָרֵיכוֹן:


Do not drink wine or any other intoxicant, you and your sons with you, when you enter the Tent of Meeting and you will not die; this is an everlasting statute throughout your generations.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:10)


וּלְאַפְרָשָׁא בֵּין קוּדְשָׁא וּבֵין חֻלָּא וּבֵין מְסָאָבָא וּבֵין דַּכְיָא:


[So that you may] distinguish between what is sacred and what is mundane, between what is impure and what is pure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:11)


וּלְאַלָּפָא יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת כָּל קְיָמַיָּא דִּי מַלִּיל יְיָ לְהוֹן בִּידָא דְמשֶׁה:


[And that you may] teach Bnei Yisroel all the statutes that Adonoy spoke to them, through Moshe.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:12)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם אַהֲרֹן וְעִם אֶלְעָזָר וְעִם אִיתָמָר בְּנוֹהִי דְּאִשְׁתָּאֲרוּ סִיבוּ יָת מִנְחָתָא דְאִשְׁתָּאָרַת **מִקֻּרְבָּנַיָּא** דַיְיָ וְאִכְלוּהָ **פַטִּיר** בִּסְטַר מַדְבְּחָא אֲרֵי קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הִיא:


Moshe spoke to Aharon and to Elozor and Isomor, his surviving sons; Take the meal-offering which remains of the fire-offering [**offerings**] of Adonoy and eat it [as] *matzos* [**matzoh**] [unleavened] beside the altar, for it is holy of holies [=most holy].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:13)


וְתֵיכְלוּן יָתַהּ בַּאֲתַר קַדִּישׁ אֲרֵי חֳלָקָךְ וָחֳלַק בְּנָיךְ הִיא **מִקֻּרְבָּנַיָּא** דַּיְיָ אֲרֵי כֵן אִתְפַּקָּדִית:


You shall eat it in a sacred place, for it is your portion and the portion of your sons of the fire-offerings [**offerings**] of Adonoy, for so have I been commanded.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:14)


וְיָת חֶדְיָא **דַאֲרָמוּתָא** וְיָת שׁוֹקָא דְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא תֵּיכְלוּן בַּאֲתַר דְּכֵי אַתְּ וּבְנָיךְ וּבְנָתָיךְ עִמָּךְ אֲרֵי חֳלָקָךְ וָחֳלַק בְּנָיךְ אִתְיְהִבוּ מִנִּכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא** דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


The breast of the wave-offering [**uplifted-offering**] and the thigh that is the separated portion you shall eat in a pure (undefiled) place, you and your sons and daughters with you, for as your portion and as the portion of your sons they are given from the sacrifices of the peace[**sacred**]-offerings of Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:15)


שׁוֹקָא דְאַפְרָשׁוּתָא וְחֶדְיָא **דַאֲרָמוּתָא** עַל קֻרְבָּנֵי תַרְבַּיָּא יַיְתוּן לַ**אֲרָמָא אֲרָמָא** קֳדָם יְיָ וִיהֵי לָךְ וְלִבְנָיךְ עִמָּךְ לִקְיַם עָלָם כְּמָא דִי פַּקִּיד יְיָ:


The thigh that is the separated portion and the breast that is the wave-offering [**uplifted-offering**], on top of the fats of the fire offering [**offering**], they shall bring, to wave [**lift up**] as a wave-offering [**an uplifted-offering**] before Adonoy. It will be for you and for your sons with you an everlasting statute, just as Adonoy commanded.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:16)


וְיָת צְפִירָא דְחַטָּתָא מִתְבַּע תָּבְעֵי משֶׁה וְהָא אִתּוֹקָד וּרְגֵז עַל אֶלְעָזָר וְעַל אִיתָמָר בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן דְּאִשְׁתָּאָרוּ לְמֵימָר:


Regarding the goat of the sin-offering; Moshe made a detailed inquiry and behold! It was burned. He was angry with Elozor and Isomor, the remaining sons of Aharon, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:17)


מָדֵין לָא אֲכַלְתּוּן יָת חַטָּאתָא בַּאֲתַר קַדִּישׁ אֲרֵי קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הִיא וְיָתַהּ יָהֵב לְכוֹן לְסַלָּחָא עַל חוֹבֵי כְנִשְׁתָּא לְכַפָּרָא עַלֵיהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ:


Why did you not eat the sin-offering in a sacred place, for it is holy of holies [=most holy], and He gave it to you, to bear the iniquity of the congregation, to atone for them before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:18)


הָא לָא אִתָּעַל יָת דְּמַהּ לְבֵית קֻדְשָׁא גַּוָּאָה מֵיכַל תֵּיכְלוּן יָתַהּ בְּקֻדְשָׁא כְּמָא דִי פַקֵּדִית:


Behold its blood was not brought inside the Sanctuary, you should certainly have eaten it in a holy place as I have commanded.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:19)


וּמַלִּיל אַהֲרֹן עִם משֶׁה הָא יוֹמָא דֵין קָרִיבוּ יָת חַטָּאתְהוֹן וְיָת עֲלָתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ וַעֲרָעָא יָתִי עָקָן כְּאִלֵּין וְאִלּוּפוֹן אֲכָלִית חַטָּאתָא יוֹמָא דֵין **הֲתַקֵּן** קֳדָם יְיָ:


Aharon spoke [in reply] to Moshe, Behold, today they brought their sin-offerings and their burnt-offerings before Adonoy and then such [tragedy] befell me. If I had eaten the sin-offering today, would that have been good in the eyes of [**fitting before**] Adonoy?


###### Onkelos Leviticus 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/10:20)


וּשְׁמַע משֶׁה **וּשְׁפַר** בְּעֵינוֹהִי:


Moshe heard, and it was good [**pleasing**] in his eyes.



## Chapter 11

###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וּלְאַהֲרֹן לְמֵימַר לְהוֹן:


And Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying to them:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:2)


מַלִּילוּ עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימָר דָּא חַיְתָא דִּי תֵיכְלוּן מִכָּל בְּעִירָא דִּי עַל אַרְעָא:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying: these are the living things that you may eat from among all the animals that are on the earth.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:3)


כָּל דִּי סְדִיקָן פַּרְסָתָא וּמַטִּלְפָן טִלְפִין פַּרְסָתָא **מַסְּקָא פִשְׁרָא** בִּבְעִירָא יָתַהּ תֵּיכְלוּן:


All that have a cloven hoof, and they [the hooves] are [completely] split and that [also] chews its cud [**brings up dissolved food**] among animals, you may eat it.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:4)


בְּרַם יָת דֵּין לָא תֵיכְלוּן **מִמַּסְּקֵי פִשְׁרָא** וּמִמַּסְדִּיקֵי פַּרְסָתָא יָת גַּמְלָא אֲרֵי **מַסִּיק פִּשְׁרָא** הוּא וּפַרְסָתָא לָא סְדִיקָא מְסָאָב הוּא לְכוֹן:


However, these in particular you may not eat of those that chew [**bring up**] their cud [**dissolved food**] and that have cloven hooves: the camel, for he chews [**brings up**] his cud [**dissolved food**] but does not have a split hoof it is unclean to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:5)


וְיָת **טַפְזָא** אֲרֵי **מַסִּיק פִּשְׁרָא** הוּא וּפַרְסָתָא לָא סְדִיקָא מְסָאָב הוּא לְכוֹן:


The [**jumping**] rabbit , for it chews [**brings up**] its cud [**dissolved food**] but does not have a cloven hoof it is unclean to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:6)


וְיָת אַרְנְבָא אֲרֵי **מַסְּקָא פִשְׁרָא** הִיא וּפַרְסָתָא לָא סְדִיקָא מְסָאָבָא הִיא לְכוֹן:


The hare, for it chews [**brings up**] its cud [**dissolved food**] but it does not have a cloven hoof it is unclean to you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:7)

The pig, for though it has cloven hooves, and its hooves are [completely] split—it does not chew [**bring up**] its cud [**dissolved food**]—it is unclean to you.

You shall not eat of their flesh and you may not touch their carcasses; they are unclean to you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:9)

וְכֹל דִּי לֵית לֵיהּ צִיצִין וְקַלְפִין בְּיַמְמַיָּא וּבְנַחֲלַיָּא מִכֹּל רִחֲשָׁא דְמַיָּא וּמִכָּל נַפְשָׁא חַיְתָא דִּי בְמַיָּא שִׁקְצָא אִנּוּן לְכוֹן:


All that do not have fins and scales [that are] in the oceans and rivers; of any creature that creeps in the water and of any living creature that is in the water; they are repulsive to you.

כֹּל דִּי לֵית לֵיהּ צִיצִין וְקַלְפִין בְּמַיָּא שִׁקְצָא הוּא לְכוֹן:

Of them, these you may eat; the locust, to its kind the *solom*-locust, to its kind, the grasshopper, to its kind and the *chogov*-hopper, to its kind.

All flying insects that have four feet are repulsive to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:24)


וּלְאִלֵּין תִּסְתָּאָבוּן כָּל דְּיִקְרַב בִּנְבִלְתְּהוֹן יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


As for these, [animals], you can be made unclean; all those who touch their carcasses shall be unclean until the evening.

Anyone who carries of their carcasses; shall wash his garments and is unclean until the evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:26)

Thus every animal that has cloven hooves but its hooves are not [completely] split and it does not bring up its cud [**dissolved food**], they are unclean to you; all who touch them shall become unclean.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:27)


וְכֹל דִּמְהַלֵּךְ עַל יְדוֹהִי בְּכָל חַיְתָא דִּמְהַלְכָא עַל אַרְבַּע מְסַאֲבִין אִנּוּן לְכוֹן כָּל דְּיִקְרַב בִּנְבִלְתְּהוֹן יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:28)


וּדְיִטּוֹל יָת נְבִלְתְּהוֹן יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא מְסַאֲבִין אִנּוּן לְכוֹן:


Whoever carries their carcasses shall wash his garments and is unclean until the evening; they are unclean to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:29)


וְדֵין לְכוֹן מְסָאָבָא בְּרִחֲשָׁא דְּרָחֵשׁ עַל אַרְעָא חֻלְדָא וְעַכְבָּרָא וְצָבָא לִזְנוֹהִי:

וְיָלָא וְכֹחָא וְחֻלְטֵיתָא וְחֻמְטָא וְאָשׁוּתָא:


The hedgehog, the chameleon, the lizard, the slug and the mole.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/11:31)

###### Onkelos Leviticus 11:32

וְכֹל דִּי יִפֵּל עֲלוֹהִי מִנְּהוֹן בְּמוֹתְהוֹן יְהֵי מְסָאָב מִכָּל מָאן דְּאָעָא אוֹ לְבוּשׁ אוֹ מְשַׁךְ אוֹ שָׂק כָּל מָאן דִּי תִתְעֲבֵד עִבִידָא בְּהוֹן בְּמַיָּא יִתָּעַל וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא וְיִדְכֵּי:
