## Chapter 12

###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:1)

Adonoy spoke to Moshe saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:2)


מַלֵּיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימַר אִתְּתָא אֲרֵי תְעַדִּי וּתְלִיד דְּכָר וּתְהֵי מְסָאָבָא שִׁבְעַת יוֹמִין כְּיוֹמֵי **רִחוּק סוֹבְתַהּ** תְּהֵי מְסָאָבָא:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel, saying, when a woman conceives and gives birth to a male child she will be ritually unclean for seven days; as the days of her menstrual flow [**the state of her impure ‘separation’**], she will be unclean.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:3)


וּבְיוֹמָא תְּמִינָאָה יִגְזַר בִּסְרָא דְעָרְלְתֵיהּ:


On the eighth day, the flesh of his foreskin shall be circumcised.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:4)


וּתְלָתִין וּתְלָתָא יוֹמִין תֵּיתֵב בְּדַם דְּכוּ בְּכָל קוּדְשָׁא לָא תִקְרַב וּלְמִקְדְּשָׁא לָא תֵעוֹל עַד מִשְׁלַם יוֹמֵי דָכוּתַהּ:


For thirty-three days she will remain [in the status] that the blood [she sees] is ritually pure. She shall not touch anything holy and she shall not come into the Sanctuary, until the days of her purity are completed.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:5)


וְאִם נְקוּבְתָא תְלִיד וּתְהֵי מְסָאָבָא **אַרְבְּעָה עֲסַר כְּרִחוּקַהּ** וְשִׁתִּין וְשִׁתָּא יוֹמִין תֵּיתֵב עַל דַּם דְּכוּ:


If she gives birth to a female she will be unclean for two weeks [**fourteen days**] as during her menstruation [**‘separation’**]. For sixty-six days she will remain [in the status] that the blood [she sees] is ritually pure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:6)


וּבְמִשְׁלַם יוֹמֵי דָכוּתַהּ לִבְרָא אוֹ לִבְרַתָּא תַּיְתִי אִמָּר בַּר שַׁתֵּיהּ לַעֲלָתָא וּבַר יוֹנָה אוֹ שַׁפְנִינָא לְחַטָּאתָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְוָת כַּהֲנָא:


When the days of her purification are completed, be it for a son or for a daughter, she shall bring a lamb, in its first year, as a burnt-offering and a young pigeon or a turtledove as a sin-offering, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, to the *kohein.*


###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:7)


וִיקָרְבִנֵּיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ וִיכַפֵּר עֲלַהּ וְתִדְכֵּי **מִסּוֹאֲבַת** דְּמָהָא דָּא אוֹרַיְתָא דְיַלֶּדְתָּא לְדוּכְרָא אוֹ לִנְקֻבְתָא:


He will bring it before Adonoy and atone for her and she will be cleansed from the source [**impurity**] of her blood; this is the law regarding the woman who gives birth to a male or to a female.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/12:8)


וְאִם לָא תַשְׁכַּח יְדַהּ כְּמִסַּת אִמְּרָא וְתִסַּב תַּרְתֵּין שַׁפְנִינִין אוֹ תְרֵין בְּנֵי יוֹנָה חַד לַעֲלָתָא וְחַד לְחַטָּאתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלַהּ כַּהֲנָא וְתִדְכֵּי:


If she cannot afford [to acquire] a lamb she shall take two turtledoves or two young pigeons; one as a burnt-offering and one as a sin-offering. The *kohein* will atone for her and she will be cleansed.



## Chapter 13

###### Onkelos Leviticus 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה וְעִם אַהֲרֹן לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:2)


אֱנַשׁ אֲרֵי יְהֵי בִמְשַׁךְ בִּסְרֵיהּ **עַמְקָא** אוֹ עַדְיָא אוֹ בַהֲרָא וִיהֵי בִמְשַׁךְ בִּסְרֵיהּ לְמַכְתַּשׁ **סְגִירוּ** וְיִתֵּתֵי לְוָת אַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא אוֹ לְוָת חַד מִבְּנוֹהִי כָּהֲנַיָּא:


When a person will have, in the skin of his flesh a spot of intense [**deep**] whiteness or an off-white spot, or a snow-white spot, and it forms on the skin of his body [like] the plague of *tzoraas,* [**‘confinement’**] he shall be brought to Aharon, the *kohein,* or to one of his sons, the *kohanim.*


###### Onkelos Leviticus 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:3)


וֶיֶחֱזֵי כַהֲנָא יָת מַכְתָּשָׁא בִמְשַׁךְ בִּסְרָא וְשֵׂעָר בְּמַכְתָּשָׁא אִתְהֲפִיךְ לְמֶחֱוָר וּמֶחֱזֵי מַכְתָּשָׁא עַמִּיק בִּמְשַׁךְ בִּסְרֵיהּ מַכְתַּשׁ **סְגִירוּתָא** הוּא וְיֶחֱזִנֵּיהּ כַּהֲנָא וִיסָאֵב יָתֵיהּ:


The *kohein* shall see the plague in the skin of the flesh and [if] the hair in the skin-eruption has turned white and the appearance of the plague is deeper than the skin of his body, it is the skin-eruption of *tzoraas.* [**‘confinement’**] The *kohein* shall see it and declare him [to be] unclean.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:4)


וְאִם בַּהֲרָא חַוְּרָא הִיא בִּמְשַׁךְ בִּסְרֵיהּ וְעַמִּיק לֵית מֶחֱזָהָא מִן מַשְׁכָא וּשְׂעָרָא לָא אִתְהֲפִיךְ לְמֶחֱוָר וְיַסְגַּר כַּהֲנָא יָת מַכְתָּשָׁא שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין:

###### Onkelos Leviticus 13:59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/13:59)


דָּא אוֹרַיְתָא דְמַכְתַּשׁ **סְגִירוּ** בִּלְבוּשׁ עֲמַר אוֹ כִתָּנָא אוֹ שִׁתְיָא אוֹ עַרְבָא אוֹ כָּל מָאן דִּמְשָׁךְ לְדַכָּיוּתֵיהּ אוֹ לְסַאֲבוּתֵיהּ:


This is the law of the eruption of *tzoraas* [**‘confinement’**] be it in a woolen garment, or a linen garment, or in the warp [lengthwise] thread, or in the woof [widthwise] thread, or in any leather-made object, that it be pronounced pure, or that it be pronounced impure.



## Chapter 14

###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


And Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:2)


דָּא תְהֵי אוֹרַיְתָא **דִסְגִירָא** בִּיוֹמָא דְדָכוּתֵיהּ וְיִתֵּתֵי לְוָת כַּהֲנָא:


This shall be the law regarding the person with *tzoraas* [**’confinement’**] on the day of his purification, he shall be brought to the *kohein*.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:3)


וְיִפּוֹק כַּהֲנָא לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וְיֶחֱזֵי כַהֲנָא וְהָא אִתַּסִּי מַכְתַּשׁ **סְגִירוּתָא** מִן **סְגִירָא**:


The *kohein* shall go out beyond the encampment. The *kohein* shall see; and behold! the skin-eruption of *tzoraas* [**’confinement’**] has healed from the person with *tzoraas* [**’confinement’**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:4)


וִיפַקֵּד כַּהֲנָא וְיִסַּב לְמִדַּכֵּי תַּרְתֵּין צִפְּרִין חַיִּין דָּכְיָן וְאָעָא דְאַרְזָא וּ**צְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וְאֵזוֹבָא:


The *kohein* shall command to take for the person undergoing purification two live, pure [kosher] birds, cedar wood, crimson [**colored**] thread and hyssop.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:5)


וִיפַקֵּד כַּהֲנָא וְיִכּוֹס יָת צִפְּרָא חֲדָא לְמַאן דַּחֲסַף עַל מֵי מַבּוּעַ:


The *kohein* shall command to slaughter the one bird in an earthenware vessel, over running water.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:6)


יָת צִפְּרָא חַיְתָא יִסַּב יָתַהּ וְיָת אָעָא דְאַרְזָא וְיָת **צְבַע** זְהוֹרִי וְיָת אֵזוֹבָא וְיִטְבּוֹל יָתְהוֹן וְיָת צִפְּרָא חַיְתָא בִּדְמָא דְּצִפְּרָא דִּנְכִיסָא עַל מֵי מַבּוּעַ:


[As for] the living bird, he shall take it; and the cedar wood, the crimson [**colored**] thread and the hyssop and immerse them and the live bird in the blood of the slaughtered bird, over the running water.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:7)


וְיַדִּי עַל דְּמִדַּכֵּי מִן **סְגִירוּתָא** שְׁבַע זִמְנִין וִידַכִּנֵּיהּ וִישַׁלַּח יָת צִפְּרָא חַיְתָא עַל אַפֵּי חַקְלָא:


He shall sprinkle on the person undergoing purification from the *tzoraas* [**’confinement’**], seven times and make him pure; and send the live bird into the open field.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:8)


וִיצַבַּע דְּמִדַּכֵּי יָת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וִיגַלַּח יָת כָּל שַׂעֲרֵיהּ וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא וְיִדְכֵּי וּבָתַר כֵּן יֵעוֹל לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וִיתֵב מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין:


The person undergoing purification shall wash his garments, and shave off all of his hair, and bathe in water, and he will become purified. After [this], he may come into the encampment. He must remain, outside of his tent, for seven days.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:9)


וִיהֵי בְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה יְגַלַּח יָת כָּל שַׂעֲרֵיהּ יָת רֵישֵׁיהּ וְיָת דִּקְנֵיהּ וְיָת גְּבִינֵי עֵינוֹהִי וְיָת כָּל שַׂעֲרֵיהּ יְגַלָּח וִיצַבַּע יָת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי יָת בִּסְרֵיהּ בְּמַיָּא וְיִדְכֵּי:


It shall be, that on the seventh day, he shall shave off all of his hair, [that of] his head, his beard and his eyebrows; he shall shave off all of his hair. He shall wash his garments, and bathe his body in water and become purified.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:10)


וּבְיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה יִסַּב תְּרֵין אִמְּרִין שַׁלְמִין וְאִמַּרְתָּא חֲדָא בַּת שַׁתָּא שְׁלֶמְתָּא וּתְלָתָא עֶסְרוֹנִין סֻלְתָּא מִנְחָתָא דְּפִילָא בִמְשַׁח וְלֻגָּא חַד דְּמִשְׁחָא:


On the eighth day, he shall take two unblemished lambs and one ewe in her first year, unblemished, and three tenths fine flour, for a meal-offering, mixed with oil and one *log* of oil.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:11)


וִיקִים כַּהֲנָא דִמְדַּכֵּי יָת גַּבְרָא דְּמִדַּכֵּי וְיָתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


The *kohein* doing the purification, shall place the man undergoing the purification and the [aforementioned] items, before Adonoy, at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:12)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא יָת אִמְּרָא חֲדָא וִיקָרֵב יָתֵיהּ לַאֲשָׁמָא וְיָת לֻגָּא דְמִשְׁחָא **וִירִים** יָתְהוֹן אֲרָמָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


The *kohein* shall take one lamb and bring it as a guilt-offering, and (with) the *log* of oil and wave [**lift**] them [**up**] as a wave-offering [**an uplifted offering**] before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:13)


וְיִכּוֹס יָת אִמְּרָא בַּאֲתַר דִּי יִכּוֹס יָת חַטָּאתָא וְיָת עֲלָתָא בַּאֲתַר קַדִּישׁ אֲרֵי כְּחַטָּאתָא אֲשָׁמָא הוּא לְכַהֲנָא קֹדֶשׁ קוּדְשִׁין הוּא:


He shall slaughter the lamb in the place where he slaughters the sin-offering and the burnt-offering, in the sacred place; for, just as the sin-offering—is the guilt-offering; it belongs to the *kohein*, it is most holy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:14)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא מִדְּמָא דַאֲשָׁמָא וְיִתֵּן כַּהֲנָא עַל **רוּם** אוּדְנָא דְמִדַּכֵּי דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן יְדֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן רַגְלֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא:


The *kohein* shall take from the blood of the guilt-offering and the *kohein* shall put it on the middle ridge [**upper part**] of the right ear of the person being purified, and on the thumb of his right hand and on the big toe of his right foot.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:15)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא מִלֻּגָּא דְמִשְׁחָא וִירִיק עַל יְדָא דְכַהֲנָא דִּשְׂמָאלָא:


The *kohein* shall take from the *log* of the oil and he shall pour it onto the koheins left palm.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:16)


וְיִטְבּוֹל כַּהֲנָא יָת אֶצְבְּעֵיהּ דְיַמִּינָא מִן מִשְׁחָא דִּי עַל יְדֵיהּ דִּשְׂמָאלָא וְיַדִּי מִן מִשְׁחָא בְּאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ שְׁבַע זִמְנִין קֳדָם יְיָ:


The *kohein* shall immerse his right finger in some of the oil that is on his left palm and he shall sprinkle some of the oil with his finger seven times before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:17)


וּמִשְּׁאָר מִשְׁחָא דִּי עַל יְדֵיהּ יִתֵּן כַּהֲנָא עַל **רוּם** אוּדְנָא דְּמִדַּכֵּי דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן יְדֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן רַגְלֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא עַל דְּמָא דַאֲשָׁמָא:


From the remaining oil that is on his palm, the *kohein* shall put on the middle ridge [**upper part**] of the right ear of the person being purified, and on the thumb of his right hand, and on the big toe of his right foot; on [over] the blood of the guilt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:18)


וּדְאִשְׁתָּאַר בְּמִשְׁחָא דִּי עַל יְדָא דְכַהֲנָא יִתֵּן עַל רֵישָׁא דְּמִדַּכֵּי וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי כַּהֲנָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Whatever oil remains that is on the palm of the *kohein*, he shall put on the head of the person undergoing purification. The *kohein* will atone for him before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:19)


וְיַעְבֵּד כַּהֲנָא יָת חַטָּאתָא וִיכַפֵּר עַל דְּמִדַּכֵּי מְסּוֹבְתֵיהּ וּבָתַר כֵּן יִכּוֹס יָת עֲלָתָא:


The *kohein* shall do (offer) the sin-offering and atone for the person undergoing purification from his impurity and afterwards he shall slaughter the burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:20)


וְיַסֵּק כַּהֲנָא יָת עֲלָתָא וְיָת מִנְחָתָא לְמַדְבְּחָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי כַּהֲנָא וְיִדְכֵּי:


The *kohein* shall raise up the burnt-offering and the meal-offering onto the altar and the *kohein* will atone for him and he will be purified.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:21)


וְאִם מִסְכֵּן הוּא וְלֵית יְדֵיהּ מַדְבְּקָא וְיִסַּב אִמַּר חַד אֲשָׁמָא **לַאֲרָמָא** לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹהִי וְעִסְרוֹנָא סֻלְתָּא חַד דְּפִיל בִּמְשַׁח לְמִנְחָא וְלֻגָּא דְמִשְׁחָא:


If he is poor and his means are not sufficient, he shall take one lamb as a guilt-offering, for a wave-offering [**an uplifted offering**] to atone for him and one tenth fine flour mixed with oil, as a meal-offering and a *log* of oil.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:22)


וְתַרְתֵּין שַׁפְנִינִין אוֹ תְרֵין בְּנֵי יוֹנָה דִּי תַדְבֵּק יְדֵיהּ וִיהֵי חַד חַטָּאתָא וְחַד עֲלָתָא:


And two turtledoves or two young pigeons such as his means suffice. One shall be for a sin-offering and one shall be for a burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:23)


וְיַיְתִי יָתְהוֹן בְּיוֹמָא תְמִינָאָה לְדַכָּיוּתֵיהּ לְוָת כַּהֲנָא לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


He shall bring them on the eighth day of his purification, to the *kohein*, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:24)


וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא יָת אִמְּרָא דַאֲשָׁמָא וְיָת לֻגָּא דְמִשְׁחָא וִירִים יָתְהוֹן כַּהֲנָא **אֲרָמָא** קֳדָם יְיָ:


The *kohein* shall take the guilt-offering sheep and the *log* of oil and the *kohein* shall wave [**lift**] them [**up**] as a wave-offering [**an uplifted offering**] before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:25)


וְיִכּוֹס יָת אִמְּרָא דַאֲשָׁמָא וְיִסַּב כַּהֲנָא מִדְּמָא דַאֲשָׁמָא וְיִתֵּן עַל **רוּם** אוּדְנָא דְמִדַּכֵּי דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן יְדֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא וְעַל אִלְיוֹן רַגְלֵיהּ דְּיַמִּינָא:


He shall slaughter the guilt-offering lamb. The *kohein* shall take some blood of the guilt-offering and put it on the middle ridge [**upper part**] of the right ear of the person undergoing purification, and on the thumb of his right hand and the big toe of his right foot.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/14:26)


וּמִן מִשְׁחָא יְרִיק כַּהֲנָא עַל יְדָא דְכַהֲנָא דִּשְׂמָאלָא:


The *kohein* shall pour some of the oil onto the koheins left palm.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 14:27

###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:1

Adonoy spoke to Moshe and to Aharon, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:2)


מַלִּילוּ עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימְרוּן לְהוֹן גְּבַר גְּבַר אֲרֵי יְהֵי דָּאִיב מִבִּשְׂרֵיהּ דּוֹבֵיהּ מְסָאָב הוּא:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them; should [discharge] flow from the body of any man, [because of] his discharge, he is impure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:3)


וְדָא תְּהֵי סוֹבְתֵיהּ בְּדוֹבֵיהּ רָר בִּשְׂרֵיהּ יָת דּוֹבֵיהּ אוֹ חָתִים בִּשְׂרֵיהּ מִדּוֹבֵיהּ סוֹבְתֵיהּ הִיא:


This is the law of his impurity, when his discharge flows; His flesh drips [with] his discharge or his flesh is stopped up by his discharge, it causes him to become impure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:4)


כָּל מִשְׁכְּבָא דִּי יִשְׁכּוּב עֲלוֹהִי דּוֹבָנָא יְהֵי מְסָאָב וְכָל מָנָא דְיֵיתֵב עֲלוֹהִי יְהֵי מְסָאָב:


Every bed [Anything that is used specifically for sleeping], upon which will lie a *zov* [a person from whom a discharge flows,] shall become impure. Every vessel upon which he sits, shall become impure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:5)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִקְרַב בּמִשְׁכְּבֵיהּ יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


A person who touches his bed shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:6)

Whoever sits on the vessel that was sat upon by a *zov* shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:7)

Whoever touches the flesh of a *zov* shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.

If the *zov* spits on a ritually clean person, he shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.

Anything that is used for riding upon which a *zov* will ride shall become impure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:10)


וְכָל דְּיִקְרַב בְּכֹל דִּי יְהֵי תְחוֹתוֹהִי יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא וּדְיִטּוֹל יָתְהוֹן יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


Anyone who touches anything which is beneath him shall be impure until the evening. and whoever carries them shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:11)


וְכֹל דִּי יִקְרַב בֵּיהּ דּוֹבָנָא וִידוֹהִי לָא שְׁטַף בְּמַיָּא וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


If a *zov* touches anyone and his hands were not rinsed in water, he shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.

An earthenware vessel which the *zov* will touch shall be broken. Any wooden vessel shall be rinsed in water.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:13)


וַאֲרֵי יִדְכֵּי דּוֹבָנָא מִדּוֹבֵיהּ וְיִמְנֵי לֵיהּ שִׁבְעַת יוֹמִין לְדָכוּתֵיהּ וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי בִשְׂרֵיהּ בְּמֵי מַבּוּעַ וְיִדְכֵּי:


When a *zov* will become ritually clean, from his discharge-impurity, he shall count for himself seven days for his purification and wash his garments, and bathe in running water, and he will be purified.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:14)

On the eighth day he shall take for himself two turtledoves or two young pigeons and come before Adonoy, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting and give them to the *kohein*.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:15)


וְיַעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן כַּהֲנָא חַד חַטָּאתָא וְחַד עֲלָתָא וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי כַּהֲנָא קֳדָם יְיָ מִדּוֹבֵיהּ:


The *kohein* shall do (offer) them, one as a sin-offering and the [other] one as a burnt-offering. The *kohein* will atone for him before Adonoy, from his discharge-impurity.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:16)


וּגְבַר אֲרֵי תִפּוֹק מִנֵּיהּ שִׁכְבַת זַרְעָא וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא יָת כָּל בִּשְׂרֵיהּ וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


A man, if semen issues from him, he shall bathe his entire body in water and is impure until the evening.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:17)


וְכָל לְבוּשׁ וְכָל מְשַׁךְ דִּי יְהֵי עֲלוֹהִי שִׁכְבַת זַרְעָא וְיִצְטַבַּע בְּמַיָּא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


Any garment and any leather object upon which there is semen shall be washed in water and is impure until the evening.

וְאִתְּתָא דִּי יִשְׁכּוּב גְּבַר יָתַהּ שִׁכְבַת זַרְעָא וְיַסְחוּן בְּמַיָּא וְיִסְאֲבוּן עַד רַמְשָׁא:


A woman, with whom a man will lie conjugally; they shall bathe in water, and are impure until the evening.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:19)


וְאִתְּתָא אֲרֵי תְהֵי דָיְבָא דָּם יְהֵי דוֹבַהּ בְּבִשְׂרַהּ שִׁבְעָא יוֹמִין תְּהֵי **בְרִיחוּקַהּ** וְכָל דְּיִקְרַב בַּהּ יְהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא:


When a woman has a discharge [and] the discharge will be blood in her body, for seven days she will be in [the state of] her menstrual impurity [**’separation’**]. Anyone who touches her will be impure until the evening.

Everything that she sleeps on, during her menstrual impurity [**’separation’**], will become impure and everything that she sits on will be impure.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:21)

Anyone who touches what she slept on, shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:22)

Anyone who touches any vessel that she sits on, shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.

If a man will lie conjugally with her, her menstrual impurity [**’separation’**] will be on him and he is impure for seven days. Anything used for sleeping—if he should sleep on it—it will be impure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:25)


וְאִתְּתָא אֲרֵי יְדוּב דּוֹב דְּמַהּ יוֹמִין סַגִּיאִין בְּלָא עִדַּן רִחוּקַהּ אוֹ אֲרֵי תְדוּב עַל **רִחוּקַהּ** כָּל יוֹמֵי דּוֹב סוֹבְתַהּ כְּיוֹמֵיהּ **רִחוּקַהּ** תְּהֵי מְסָאֳבָא הִיא:


When a woman discharges her blood-flow, many days outside her menstrual cycle [**period of ‘separation’**], or if it flows after her menstrual cycle [**’separation’**], all the days of her discharge-impurity shall be as her days of menstrual impurity [**’separation’**]; she is impure.

Anything used for sleeping upon which she will lie [on] any of her discharge-impurity-days, as the bed of her menstrual impurity [**’separation’**] it shall be to her. Every vessel upon which she sits will be impure, just as [is] her menstrual-impurity [**the impurity of her ‘separation’**].

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:27)

Anyone that touches them will be impure and he shall wash his garments and bathe in water and is impure until the evening.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:28

When she has become pure from her discharge-impurity, she shall count seven days for herself and afterwards she becomes purified.
