The *kohein* shall do (offer) one as a sin-offering and the [other] one as a burnt-offering. The *kohein* will atone for her before Adonoy from her discharge-impurity.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:31)


וְתַפְרְשׁוּן יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִסּוֹאֲבַתְהוֹן וְלָא יְמוּתוּן בְּסוֹאֲבַתְהוֹן בְּסָאָבְהוֹן יָת מַשְׁכְּנִי דִּי בֵינֵיהוֹן:


You shall separate Bnei Yisroel from their impurities and they will not die because of their impurities through their defilement of My dwelling in their midst.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:32)


דָּא אוֹרַיְתָא דְּדוֹבְנָא וְדִי תִפּוֹק מִנֵּיהּ שִׁכְבַת זַרְעָא לְאִסְתָּאָבָא בַהּ:


This is the law of the *zov* and of him from whom semen issues to become impure through it.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 15:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/15:33)


**וְלִדְסָאוּבְתָּא בְּרִיחוּקַהּ** וְלִדְדָאִיב יָת דּוֹבֵיהּ לִדְכַר וּלְנֻקְבָּא וְלִגְבַר דִּי יִשְׁכּוּב עִם מְסָאָבָא:


For the menstruant [**woman who is impure**] during her period of separation and for one who has a discharge for a male and for a female; and for the man that will lie conjugally with an impure woman.



## Chapter 16

###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בָּתַר דְּמִיתוּ תְּרֵין בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן בְּקָרוֹבֵיהוֹן **אֶשָּׁתָא נוּכְרֵיתָא** קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִיתוּ:


And Adonoy spoke to Moshe after the death of two sons of Aharon, who brought an [unauthorized] offering [**strange fire**] before Adonoy and they died.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:2)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה מַלֵּל עִם אַהֲרֹן אָחוּךְ וְלָא יְהֵי עָלֵל בְּכָל עִדָּן לְקוּדְשָׁא מִגָּיו לְפָרֻכְתָּא לָקֳדָם כַּפֻּרְתָּא דִּי עַל אֲרוֹנָא וְלָא יְמוּת אֲרֵי בַּעֲנָנָא אֲנָא **מִתְגְּלִי** עַל **בֵּית** כַּפֻּרְתָּא:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe: Speak to your brother Aharon that he not come at all times into the Holy [Sanctuary] that is inside of the Curtain, before the Ark-cover that is on the Ark so that he not die, for in a cloud I shall appear [**become revealed**] on the [**House of the**] Ark-cover.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:3)


בְּדָא יְהֵי עָלֵל אַהֲרֹן לְקוּדְשָׁא בְּתוֹר בַּר תּוֹרֵי לְחַטָּאתָא וּדְכַר לַעֲלָתָא:


With this shall Aharon come into the Holy [Sanctuary]; with a young bullock, for a sin-offering and a ram, for a burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:4)


כִּתּוּנָא דְבוּצָא קוּדְשָׁא יִלְבָּשׁ וּמִכְנְסִין דְּבוּץ יְהוֹן עַל בִּסְרֵיהּ וּבְהֶמְיָנָא דְבוּצָא יֵסַר וּבְמִצְנֶפְתָּא דְבוּצָא יָחֵת בְּרֵישֵׁיהּ לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא אִנּוּן וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא יָת בִּסְרֵיהּ וְיַלְבְּשִׁנּוּן:


He shall don a sanctified, linen tunic and linen pants shall be on his body. He shall gird himself with a linen sash and place a linen turban on his head. These are sacred garments; he shall bathe his body in water and clothe himself in them.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:5)


וּמִין כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יִסַּב תְּרֵין צְפִירֵי עִזִּין לְחַטָּאתָא וּדְכַר חַד לַעֲלָתָא:


He shall take, from the congregation of Israel two he-goats for sin-offerings and one ram for a burnt-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:6)


וִיקָרֵב אַהֲרֹן יָת תּוֹרָא דְחַטָּאתָא דִּי לֵיהּ וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי וְעַל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ:


Aharon shall bring the sin-offering bullock which is his and atone on his own behalf and on behalf of his household.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:7)


וְיִסַּב יָת תְּרֵין צְפִירִין וִיקִים יָתְהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא:


He shall take the two he-goats and stand them before Adonoy, at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:8)


וְיִתֵּן אַהֲרֹן עַל תְּרֵין צְפִירִין עַדְבִין עַדְבָא חַד **לִשְׁמָא** דַיְיָ וְעַדְבָא חַד לַעֲזָאזֵל:


Aharon shall put lots on the two he-goats; one lot [marked] for [**the Name of**] Adonoy and one lot [marked] for Azazel.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:9)


וִיקָרֵב אַהֲרֹן יָת צְפִירָא דִּי סְלִיק עֲלוֹהִי עַדְבָא **לִשְׁמָא** דַיְיָ וְיַעְבִּדִנֵיהּ חַטָּאתָא:


Aharon shall bring the goat upon which the lot [marked] for [**the Name of**] Adonoy came up and do (offer) it as a sin-offering.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:10)


וּצְפִירָא דִּי סְלִיק עֲלוֹהִי עַדְבָא לַעֲזָאזֵל יִתָּקַם כַּד חַי קֳדָם יְיָ לְכַפָּרָא עֲלוֹהִי לְשַׁלַּח יָתֵיהּ לַעֲזָאזֵל לְמַדְבְּרָא:


The goat upon which came up the lot [marked] for Azazel shall be placed, alive, before Adonoy, to achieve atonement with it to send it to Azazel, in the desert.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:11)


וִיקָרֵב אַהֲרֹן יָת תּוֹרָא דְחַטָּאתָא דִּי לֵיהּ וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי וְעַל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ וְיִכּוֹס יָת תּוֹרָא דְחַטָּאתָא דִּי לֵיהּ:


Aharon shall bring the sin-offering-bullock which is his and atone on his behalf and on behalf of his household, and he shall slaughter his sin-offering-bullock.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:12)


וְיִסַּב מְלֵי מַחְתִּיתָא גּוּמְרִין דְּאֶשָּׁא מֵעִלַּוֵי מַדְבְּחָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּמְלֵי חָפְנוֹהִי קְטוֹרֶת **בּוּסְמִין** דַּקִּיקִין וְיָעֵל מִגָּיו לְפָרֻכְתָּא:


He shall take a full hand-shovel of fiery coals from atop the altar, which is before Adonoy and full handfuls of finely-ground incense and he shall bring [them] inside of the curtain.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:13)


וְיִתֵּן יָת קְטוֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָּא עַל אֶשָּׁתָא לָקֳדָם יְיָ וְחָפֵי עֲנָנָא קְטָרְתָּא יָת כַּפֻּרְתָּא דִּי עַל סַהֲדוּתָא וְלָא יְמוּת:


He shall put the incense [**spices**] on the fire before Adonoy and the cloud of incense will cover the Ark-cover that is on the [tablets of] Testimony, that he not die.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:14)


וְיִסַּב מִדְּמָא דְתוֹרָא וְיַדִּי בְאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ עַל אַפֵּי כַּפֻּרְתָּא קִדּוּמָא וְלָקֳדָם כַּפֻּרְתָּא יַדִּי שְׁבַע זִמְנִין מִן דְּמָא בְּאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ:


He shall take some of the bullocks blood and he shall sprinkle with his finger, above the surface of the Ark-cover on its eastern part; and before the Ark-cover, he shall sprinkle, some blood, with his finger, seven times.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:15)


וְיִכּוֹס יָת צְפִירָא דְחַטָּאתָא דִּי לְעַמָּא וְיָעֵל יָת דְּמֵיהּ לְמִגָּיו לְפָרֻכְתָּא וְיַעְבֵּד לִדְמֵיהּ כְּמָא דִי עֲבַד לִדְמָא דְתוֹרָא וְיַדִּי יָתֵיהּ עַל כַּפֻּרְתָּא וְלָקֳדָם כַּפֻּרְתָּא:


He shall slaughter the he-goat sin-offering that belongs to the people, and he shall bring its blood inside of the Curtain. He shall do [with] its blood just as he did with the bullocks blood and he shall sprinkle it on the Ark-cover and before of the Ark-cover.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:16)


וִיכַפֵּר עַל קוּדְשָׁא מִסּוֹאֲבַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִמֶּרְדֵּיהוֹן לְכָל חֲטָאֵיהוֹן וְכֵן יַעְבֵּד לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא דְּשָׁרֵי עִמְּהוֹן בְּגוֹ סוֹאֲבָתְהוֹן:


He will atone for the Sanctuary, from the impurities of Bnei Yisroel and from their rebellious sins for all their transgressions. He shall do likewise for the Tent of Meeting, that dwells with them in the midst of their impurity.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:17)


וְכָל אֱנַשׁ לָא יְהֵי בְּמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא בְּמֵעֲלֵיהּ לְכַפָּרָא בְקוּדְשָׁא עַד מִפְּקֵיהּ וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי וְעַל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ וְעַל כָּל קְהָלָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


No man shall be present in the Tent of Meeting when he comes in to atone for the Holy [Sanctuary] until he leaves. He shall atone on his behalf and on behalf of his household and on behalf of the entire assembly of Israel.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:18)


וְיִפּוֹק לְמַדְבְּחָא דִּי קֳדָם יְיָ וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי וְיִסַּב מִדְּמָא דְתוֹרָא וּמִדְּמָא דִצְפִירָא וְיִתֵּן עַל קַרְנַת מַדְבְּחָא סְחוֹר סְחוֹר:


He shall go out to the altar that is before Adonoy and atone on it. He shall take some of the bullocks blood and some of the he-goats blood and put it atop the corners of the altar, all around.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:19)


וְיַדֵּי עֲלוֹהִי מִן דְּמָא בְּאֶצְבְּעֵיהּ שְׁבַע זִמְנִין וִידַכִּנֵּיהּ וִיקַדְּשִׁנֵּיהּ מִסּוֹאֲבַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


He shall sprinkle on it from the blood that is on his finger seven times and purify it and sanctify it from the impurities of Bnei Yisroel.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:20)


וִישֵׁצֵי מִלְּכַפָּרָא עַל קוּדְשָׁא וְעַל מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְעַל מַדְבְּחָא וִיקָרֵב יָת צְפִירָא חַיָּא:


When he finishes atoning for the Holy [Sanctuary] and [for] the Tent of Meeting and [for] the altar he shall bring the live he-goat.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:21)


וְיִסְמוֹךְ אַהֲרֹן יָת תַּרְתֵּין יְדוֹהִי עַל רֵישׁ צְפִירָא חַיָּא וְיִוַדֵּי עֲלוֹהִי יָת כָּל עֲוָיַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְיָת כָּל מֶרְדֵּיהוֹן לְכָל חֲטָאֵיהוֹן וְיִתֵּן יָתְהוֹן עַל רֵישׁ צְפִירָא וִישַׁלַּח בְּיַד גְּבַר דִּזְמִין **לִמְהַךְ** לְמַדְבְּרָא:


Aharon shall lay his two hands on the head of the live he-goat and confess on it all the iniquities of Bnei Yisroel and all their rebellious transgressions for all their sins, and put them on the head of the goat and send it away with the man [so] designated, to [**go to**] the desert.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:22)


וְיִטּוֹל צְפִירָא עֲלוֹהִי יָת כָּל חוֹבֵיהוֹן לְאַרְעָא **דְלָא יָתְבָא** וִישַׁלַּח יָת צְפִירָא בְּמַדְבְּרָא:


The goat shall carry upon itself all of their iniquities to a desolate [**an uninhabited**] land, and [the man shall] send the goat into the desert.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:23)


וְיֵיעוֹל אַהֲרֹן לְמַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיַשְׁלַח יָת לְבוּשֵׁי בוּצָא דִּי לְבַשׁ בְּמֵעֲלֵיהּ לְקוּדְשָׁא **וְיַצְנְעִנּוּן** תַּמָּן:


Aharon will [then] come into the Tent of Meeting and remove the linen garments that he wore when he came into the Holy [Sanctuary] and place [**hide**] them there.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:24)


וְיַסְחֵי יָת בִּסְרֵיהּ בְּמַיָּא בַּאֲתַר קַדִּישׁ וְיִלְבַּשׁ יָת לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיִפּוֹק וְיַעְבֵּד יָת עֲלָתֵיהּ וְיָת עֲלַת עַמָּא וִיכַפַּר עֲלוֹהִי וְעַל עַמָּא:


He shall wash his flesh in water in a sacred place and don his garments. He shall go out and do (offer) his burnt-offering and the peoples burnt-offering and atone on his behalf and on behalf of the people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:25)


וְיָת תַּרְבָּא דְחַטָּאתָא יַסֵּק לְמַדְבְּחָא:


The fat of the sin-offering, he shall burn on the altar.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:26)


**וּדְמוֹבִיל** יָת צְפִירָא לַעֲזָאזֵל יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי יָת בִּסְרֵיהּ בְּמַיָּא וּבָתַר כֵּן יֵיעוֹל לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


He who sends [**brings**] the goat to Azazel shall wash his garments, and bathe his body in water, and afterwards he shall come into the encampment.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:27)


וְיָת תּוֹרָא דְחַטָּאתָא וְיָת צְפִירָא דְחַטָּאתָא דִּי אִתָּעַל מִדִּמְהוֹן לְכַפָּרָא בְקוּדְשָׁא יִתַּפְּקוּן לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וְיוֹקְדוּן בְּנוּרָא יָת מַשְׁכֵּיהוֹן וְיָת בִּסְרְהוֹן וְיָת אָכְלֵיהוֹן:


The sin-offering-bullock and the sin-offering-goat whose blood was brought to achieve atonement in the Holy [Sanctuary], he shall have removed beyond the encampment. They shall burn in fire their skins, their flesh and their waste [that are in their intestines].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:28)


וּדְמוֹקִיד יָתְהוֹן יְצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי יָת בִּסְרֵיהּ בְּמַיָּא וּבָתַר כֵּן יֵיעוֹל לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


He who burns them shall wash his garments and bathe his body in water and afterwards he shall come into the encampment.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:29)


וּתְהֵי לְכוֹן לִקְיַם עָלָם בְּיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה בְּעַשְׂרָא לְיַרְחָא תְּעַנּוּן יָת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן וְכָל עִבִידָא לָא תַעְבְּדוּן **יַצִּיבָא** וְגִיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיְּרוּן** בֵּינֵיכוֹן:


It shall be for an everlasting statute for you; in the seventh month, on the tenth of the month, you shall afflict yourselves and not do any work, the native-born [**settled one**] and the proselyte residing [**that converts**] among you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:30)


אֲרֵי בְיוֹמָא הָדֵין יְכַפֵּר עֲלֵיכוֹן לְדַכָּאָה יָתְכוֹן מִכֹּל חוֹבֵיכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ תִּדְכּוּן:


For on this day He will make atonement for you, to purify you from all your sins, before Adonoy, you will be purified.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:31)


שְׁבַת שְׁבָתָא הִיא לְכוֹן וּתְעַנּוּן יָת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן קְיַם עֲלָם:


It is a *Shabbos* of Shabbosos to you and you must afflict yourselves; it is an everlasting statute.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:32)


וִיכַפַּר כַּהֲנָא **דִּי רַבִּי** יָתֵיהּ וְדִי **יְקָרֵב** יָת קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ לְשַׁמָּשָׁא תְּחוֹת אֲבוּהִי וְיִלְבַּשׁ יָת לְבוּשֵׁי בוּצָא לְבוּשֵׁי קוּדְשָׁא:


The *kohein* will atone—he who himself will be anointed [**inaugurated to greatness**] and who will be initiated—[**offer his sacrifice**] to serve in his fathers stead and dress in the linen garments, the sacred garments.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:33)


וִיכַפַּר עַל מִקְדַּשׁ קוּדְשָׁא וְעַל מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְעַל מַדְבְּחָא יְכַפַּר וְעַל כַּהֲנַיָּא וְעַל כָּל עַמָּא דִקְהָלָא יְכַפַּר:


He will atone for the Holy [Sanctuary]; and for the Tent of Meeting and for the altar, he will atone; for the *kohanim* and for the entire people of the congregation, he will atone.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 16:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/16:34)


וּתְהֵי דָא לְכוֹן לִקְיַם עָלָם לְכַפָּרָא עַל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מִכָּל חוֹבֵיהוֹן חֲדָא בְּשַׁתָּא וַעֲבַד כְּמָא דִי פַקֵּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


This shall be for you as an everlasting statute, to achieve atonement for Bnei Yisroel from all their sins, once a year. He did as Adonoy commanded Moshe.



## Chapter 17

###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:2)


מַלֵּל עִם אַהֲרֹן וְעִם בְּנוֹהִי וְעִם כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן דֵּין פִּתְגָּמָא דִּי פַקִּיד יְיָ לְמֵימָר:


Speak to Aharon and to his sons and to all Bnei Yisroel and say to them; this is the edict that Adonoy commanded, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:3)


גְּבַר גְּבַר מִבֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי יִכּוֹס תּוֹר אוֹ אִמַּר אוֹ עִזָּא בְּמַשְׁרִיתָא אוֹ דִּי יִכּוֹס מִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא:


Each and every person of the House of Israel who will slaughter an ox, lamb, or goat within the encampment or who will slaughter [it] beyond the encampment.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:4)


וְלִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָא אַיְתֵיהּ לְקָרָבָא קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם יְיָ **קֳדָם** מַשְׁכְּנָא דַיְיָ דְּמָא יִתְחַשַּׁב לְגַבְרָא הַהוּא דְּמָא אֲשַׁד וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי אֲנָשָׁא הַהוּא מִגּוֹ עַמֵּיהּ:


And to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting he did not bring it so that it may be brought as an offering to [**before**] Adonoy, before the Mishkan of Adonoy; it shall be considered for that person as blood, he has spilled blood, and that person shall be cut off from among his people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:5)


בְּדִיל דִּי יַיְתוּן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת דִּבְחֵיהוֹן דִּי אִנּוּן דָּבְחִין עַל אַפֵּי חַקְלָא וְיַיְתֻנּוּן **לָקֳדָם** יְיָ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לְוָת כַּהֲנָא וְיִכְּסוּן נִכְסַת **קוּדְשִׁין קֳדָם** יְיָ יָתְהוֹן:


So that Bnei Yisroel shall bring their sacrifices that they [would] slaughter on the field, and [instead] they shall bring them to [**before**] Adonoy to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, to the *kohein*, and slaughter them as peace[**sacred**]-offerings to [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:6)


וְיִזְרוֹק כַּהֲנָא יָת דְּמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא דַיְיָ בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא וְיַסֵּק תַּרְבָּא **לְאִתְקַבָּלָא בְרַעֲוָא** קֳדָם יְיָ:


The *kohein* shall sprinkle the blood on the altar of Adonoy at the entrance of the Tent of Meeting and burn the fat as a pleasing fragrance to [**to be accepted with favor before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:7)


וְלָא יִדְבְּחוּן עוֹד יָת דִּבְחֵיהוֹן לַשֵּׁדִין דִּי אִנּוּן טָעָן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן קְיַם עָלָם תְּהֵי דָא לְהוֹן לְדָרֵיהוֹן:


They shall no longer slaughter their sacrifices to the demons after which they go astray. This shall be an everlasting statute for them, for their descendants.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:8)


וּלְהוֹן תֵּימַר גְּבַר גְּבַר מִבֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִן גִּיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיְּרוּן** בֵּינֵיכוֹן דִּי יַסֵּק עֲלָתָא אוֹ נִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא**:


To them you shall say: each and every person of the House of Israel and from the proselyte who will reside [**convert**] among them, who will bring a burnt-offering or sacrifice [**sacred-offering**],


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:9)


וְלִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא לָא אַיְתִנֵּיהּ לְמֶעְבַּד יָתֵיהּ **קֳדָם** יְיָ וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי אֲנָשָׁא הַהוּא מֵעַמֵּיהּ:


and to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting he does not bring it, that it be offered to [**before**] Adonoy; that person shall be cut off from his people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:10)


וּגְבַר גְּבַר מִבֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִן גִּיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיְּרוּן** בֵּינֵיכוֹן דִּי יֵיכוּל כָּל דְּמָא וְאֶתֵּן רוּגְזִי **בַּאֲנָשָׁא** דְּיֵכוּל יָת דְּמָא וֶאֱשֵׁיצֵי יָתֵיהּ מִגּוֹ עַמֵּיהּ:


Each and every person of the House of Israel and from the proselyte who resides [**converts**] among them who will eat any blood; I shall direct my anger on the soul of him [**person**] who eats the blood and I will cut it off from among its people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:11)


אֲרֵי נְפַשׁ בִּסְרָא בִּדְמָא הִיא וַאֲנָא יְהָבִתֵּיהּ לְכוֹן עַל מַדְבְּחָא לְכַפָּרָא עַל נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי דְמָא הוּא עַל נַפְשָׁא מְכַפֵּר:


For [the] soul of the body is in the blood and I have given it to you [to be placed] on the altar to achieve atonement for your souls, for it is the blood that will achieve atonement for the soul.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:12)


עַל כֵּן אֲמָרִית לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל כָּל **אֱנַשׁ** מִנְּכוֹן לָא יֵיכוּל דְּמָא וְגִיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיְּרוּן** בֵּינֵיכוֹן לָא יֵיכוּל דְּמָא:


Therefore, I have said to Bnei Yisroel: Every soul [**person**] among you shall not eat blood. The proselyte residing [**that converts**] among you shall not eat blood.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:13)


וּגְבַר גְּבַר מִן בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִין גִּיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיְּרוּן** בֵּינֵיכוֹן דִּי יְצוּד צֵידָא חַיְתָא אוֹ עוֹפָא דִּי מִתְאֲכֵל וְיֵישׁוֹד יָת דְּמֵיהּ וִיכַסִּנֵּיהּ בְּעַפְרָא:


Each and every person of the House of Israel and from the proselyte residing [**that converts**] among them who will ensnare [the catch] any wild animal or fowl that is permitted to be eaten, he shall spill its blood and cover it with earth.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:14)


אֲרֵי נְפַשׁ כָּל בִּסְרָא דְּמֵיהּ בְּנַפְשֵׁיהּ הוּא וַאֲמָרִית לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דַּם כָּל בִּסְרָא לָא תֵיכְלוּן אֲרֵי נְפַשׁ כָּל בִּסְרָא דְּמֵיהּ הִיא כָּל דְּיֵכְלִנֵּיהּ יִשְׁתֵּיצֵי:


For [as to] the life of all flesh, its blood is its life, and I have said to Bnei Yisroel: The blood of any flesh, you should not eat, for, the life of all flesh is its blood. Whoever eats of it, shall be cut off.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:15)


וְכָל אֱנַשׁ דִּי יֵיכוּל נְבִילָא וּתְבִירָא **בְּיַצִּיבָא** וּבְגִיּוֹרָא וִיצַבַּע לְבוּשׁוֹהִי וְיַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא וִיהֵי מְסָאָב עַד רַמְשָׁא וְיִדְכֵּי:


Anybody that will eat an improperly slaughtered animal or an animal with a fatal defect among the native-born [**settled one**] and the proselyte, shall wash his clothing and bathe in water and is impure until the evening, and [then] becomes purified.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/17:16)


וְאִם לָא יְצַבַּע וּבִסְרֵיהּ לָא יַסְחֵי וִיקַבֵּל חוֹבֵיהּ:


If he does not wash [his clothes] and does not bathe his flesh, he bears [the burden of] his iniquity.



## Chapter 18

###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:2)


מַלֵּל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:3)


כְּעוֹבָדֵי **עַמָּא** דְאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם דִּי יְתֶבְתּוּן בַּהּ לָא תַעְבְּדוּן וּכְעוֹבָדֵי **עַמָּא** דְאַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן דִּי אֲנָא מָעֵל יָתְכוֹן תַּמָּן לָא תַעְבְּדוּן וּבְנִימוֹסֵיהוֹן לָא תְהָכוּן:


After the practice of the [**people of the**] Land of Egypt in which you have lived, you shall not do, and the practice of the [**people of the**] Land of Canaan, to which I am bringing you, you shall not do and you shall not follow their statutes [customs].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:4)


יָת דִּינַי תַּעְבְּדוּן וְיָת קְיָמַי תִּטְּרוּן לְהַלָּכָא בְּהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


You shall fulfill My laws and you shall keep My statutes to follow them, I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:5)


וְתִטְּרוּן יָת קְיָמַי וְיָת דִּינַי דִּי יַעְבֵּד יָתְהוֹן אֲנָשָׁא וְיֵחֵי בְהוֹן **לְחַיֵּי עָלְמָא** אֲנָא יְיָ:


You shall keep My statutes and My laws which if a man obeys he shall live through them [**in the World to Come**]; I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:6)


גְּבַר גְּבַר לְכָל קָרִיב בִּסְרֵיהּ לָא תִקְרְבוּן לְגַלָּאָה עֶרְיָא אֲנָא יְיָ:


Each and every person—to any of his close kin—You shall not approach to uncover [their] nakedness, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:7)


עֶרְיַת אֲבוּךְ וְעֶרְיַת אִמָּךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי אִמָּךְ הִיא לָא תְגַלֵּי עֶרְיְתַהּ:


The nakedness of your father and the nakedness of your mother you shall not uncover; she is your mother, you shall not uncover her nakedness.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:8)


עֶרְיַת אִתַּת אָבוּךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי עֶרְיַת אֲבוּךְ הִיא:


The nakedness of your fathers wife you shall not uncover; it is your fathers nakedness.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:9)


עֶרְיַת אֲחָתָךְ בַּת אֲבוּךְ אוֹ בַת אִמָּךְ דִּילִידָא **מִן אֲבוּךְ מִן אִתַּת אָחֳרִי** אוֹ **מִן אִמָּךְ לִגְבַר אָחֳרָן** לָא תְגַלֵּי עֶרְיַתְהֶן:


The nakedness of your sister [whether she is] your fathers daughter or your mothers daughter; whether she is born in the house [**to your father from another woman**], or she is born outside [**to your mother from another man**], you shall not uncover their nakedness.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:10)


עֶרְיַת בַּת בְּרָךְ אוֹ בַת בְּרַתָּךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי עֶרְיַתְהֵן אֲרֵי עֶרְיְתָךְ אִנּוּן:


The nakedness of your sons daughter or your daughters daughter; you shall not uncover their nakedness, for their nakedness is your own.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:11)


עֶרְיַת בַּת אִתַּת אֲבוּךְ דִּילִידָא מִן אֲבוּךְ אֲחָתָךְ הִיא לָא תְגַלֵּי עֶרְיְתַהּ:


The nakedness of the daughter of your fathers wife who is born to your father; she is your sister, you shall not uncover her nakedness.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:12)


עֶרְיַת אֲחַת אֲבוּךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי קָרִיבַת אֲבוּךְ הִיא:


The nakedness of your fathers sister you shall not uncover; she is your fathers kin.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:13)


עֶרְיַת אֲחַת אִמָּךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי אֲרֵי קָרִיבַת אִמָּךְ הִיא:


The nakedness of your mothers sister you shall not uncover, for she is your mothers kin.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:14)


עֶרְיַת אַח אֲבוּךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי לְאִתְּתֵיהּ לָא תִקְרַב **אִתַּת אַח אֲבוּךְ** הִיא:


The nakedness of your fathers brother you shall not uncover. You shall not approach his wife, she is your aunt [**the wife of your father’s brother**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:15)


עֶרְיַת כַּלְּתָךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי אִתַּת בְּרָךְ הִיא לָא תְגַלֵּי עֶרְיְתַהּ:


The nakedness of your daughter-in-law you shall not uncover; she is your sons wife, you shall not uncover her nakedness.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:16)


עֶרְיַת אִתַּת אֲחוּךְ לָא תְגַלֵּי עֶרְיַת אֲחוּךְ הִיא:


The nakedness of your brothers wife you shall not uncover; it is your brothers nakedness.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:17)


עֶרְיַת אִתְּתָא וּבְרַתַּהּ לָא תְגַלֵּי יָת בַּת בְּרַהּ וְיָת בַּת בְּרַתַּהּ לָא תִסַּב לְגַלָּאָה עֶרְיְתַהּ קָרִיבָן אִנּוּן **עֵצַת חִטְאִין** הִיא:


The nakedness of a woman and her daughter you shall not uncover. The daughter of her son and the daughter of her daughter you shall not take to uncover her nakedness, they are kin; it is lewdness [**a sinful plan**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:18)


וְאִתְּתָא עִם אֲחָתַהּ לָא תִסָּב לְעָקָאָה לְגַלָּאָה עֶרְיְתַהּ עֲלַהּ בְּחַיָּיהָא:


You shall not take a woman and her sister to vex [her] to uncover her nakedness with the other, in her lifetime.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:19)


וּלְאִתְּתָא **בְּרִחוּק סוֹבְתַהּ** לָא תִקְרַב לְגַלָּאָה עֶרְיְתַהּ:


To a woman while in her menstrual impurity [**the state of her impure ‘separation’**], you shall not approach to uncover her nakedness.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:20)


וּבְאִתַּת חַבְרָךְ לָא תִתֵּן שְׁכֻבְתָּךְ לְזַרְעָא לְאִסְתָּאָבָא בַהּ:


With your friends wife you shall not lie carnally, to defile yourself through her.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:21)


וּמִבְּנָיךְ לָא תִתֵּן לְאַעְבָּרָא לְמוֹלֶךְ וְלָא תַחֵל יָת שְׁמָא דֶאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ:


You shall not hand over any of your children to be passed through [the worship of] *Molech* and you shall not defile the Name of your God, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:22)


וְעִם דְּכוּרָא לָא תִשְׁכּוּב מִשְׁכְּבֵי אִתְּתָא תּוֹעֶבְתָּא הִיא:


You shall not lie with a male [conjugally] as one lies with a woman; it is an abomination.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:23)


וּבְכָל בְּעִירָא לָא תִתֵּן שְׁכֻבְתָּךְ לְאִסְתָּאָבָא בַהּ וְאִתְּתָא לָא תְקוּם קֳדָם בְּעִירָא **לְמִשְׁלַט בַּהּ** תִּבְלָא הוּא:


Together with any animal you shall not lie [conjugally] to defile yourself with it. A woman shall not stand before an animal to copulate with it [**control her**]; it is defilement.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:24)


לָא תִסְתַּאֲבוּן בְּכָל אִלֵּין אֲרֵי בְכָל אִלֵּין אִסְתָּאָבוּ עַמְמַיָּא דִּי אֲנָא **מַגְלֵי** מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן:


Do not defile yourselves in any one of these [ways], for in all of these [ways] the nations have become defiled [those] that I am driving away [**exiling**] from before you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:25)


וְאִסְתָּאָבַת אַרְעָא וְאַסְעָרִית חוֹבַהּ עֲלַהּ וְרוֹקֵינַת אַרְעָא יָת יָתְבָהָא:


The land became defiled and I judged its iniquities upon it and the land expelled its inhabitants.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:26)


וְתִטְּרוּן אַתּוּן יָת קְיָמַי וְיָת דִּינַי וְלָא תַעְבְּדוּן מִכֹּל תּוֹעֶבְתָּא הָאִלֵּין **יַצִּיבָא** וְגִיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיְּרוּן** בֵּינֵיכוֹן:


You shall adhere to My statutes and to My laws and not do any of these abominations [both] the native born [**settled one**] and the proselyte who resides [**converts**] among you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:27)


אֲרֵי יָת כָּל תּוֹעֶבְתָּא הָאִלֵּין עֲבָדוּ אֱנָשֵׁי אַרְעָא דִּי קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְאִסְתָּאָבַת אַרְעָא:


For all of these abominations were done by the people of the land who came before you and the land became defiled.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:28)


וְלָא תְרוֹקֵן אַרְעָא יָתְכוֹן בְּסָאֳבֵיכוֹן יָתַהּ כְּמָא דְרוֹקֵינַת יָת **עַמְמַיָּא** דִּי קֳדָמֵיכוֹן:


[So that] the land not expel you when you defile it as it expelled the nation [**nations**] before you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:29)


אֲרֵי כָּל דִּי יַעְבֵּד מִכֹּל תּוֹעֶבְתָּא הָאִלֵּין וְיִשְׁתֵּיצוּן נַפְשָׁתָא דִיַעְבְּדוּן מִגּוֹ עַמְּהוֹן:


For anyone who will do any of these abominations, their souls shall be cut off, those who do these things, from among their people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/18:30)


וְתִטְּרוּן יָת **מַטְּרַת מֵימְרִי** בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְמֶעְבַּד מִנִּימוֹסֵי תוֹעֶבְתָּא דְּאִתְעֲבָדָא קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְלָא תִסְתַּאֲבוּן בְּהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


You shall keep my watch [**the watch of My Word**] that you shall not do any of the abominable customs that were done before you and you will not defile yourselves through them. I am Adonoy, your God.



## Chapter 19

###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:2)


מַלֵּל עִם כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן קַדִּישִׁין תְּהוֹן אֲרֵי קַדִּישׁ אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


Speak to the entire congregation [of] Bnei Yisroel and say to them: You shall be holy, for I, Adonoy, your God, am holy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:3)


גְּבַר מִן אִמֵּיהּ וּמִן אֲבוּהִי תְּהוֹן דָּחֲלִין וְיָת **יוֹמֵי** שַׁבַּיָּא דִילִי תִּטְרוּן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


A man—you shall fear his mother and father and keep [**the days of**] My Sabbaths, I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:4)


לָא תִתְפְּנוּן בָּתַר **טַעֲוָן** וְדַחֲלָן דְּמַתְּכָא לָא תַעְבְּדוּן לְכוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


Do not turn to the [**false**] idols and do not make molten gods for yourselves, I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:5)


וַאֲרֵי תִכְּסוּן נִכְסַת **קוּדְשַׁיָּא קֳדָם** יְיָ לְרַעֲוָא לְכוֹן תִּכְּסֻנֵּיהּ:


When you slaughter a peace[**sacred**]-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, you shall slaughter it to be acceptable for you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:6)


בְּיוֹמָא דְיִתְנְכֵס יִתְאֲכֵל וּבְיוֹמָא דְבַתְרוֹהִי וּדְאִשְׁתָּאַר עַד יוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה בְּנוּרָא יִתּוֹקָד:


On the day that it is slaughtered it may be eaten and on the next day. Whatever is left over by the third day shall be burned in fire.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:7)


וְאִם אִתְאֲכָלָא יִתְאֲכֵל בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה **מְרָחָק** הוּא לָא יְהֵי לְרַעֲוָא:


And if it should be eaten at all on the third day it is an abomination [**a rejection**], it will not be favorably accepted.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:8)


וּדְיֵיכְלִנֵּיהּ חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּל אֲרֵי יָת קוּדְשָׁא דַיְיָ אַחֵל וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי אֲנָשָׁא הַהוּא מְעַמֵּיהּ:


Whoever eats from it shall bear his iniquity, for the sacred [things] of Adonoy he has defiled. That [persons] soul shall be cut off from its people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:9)


וּבְמֶחְצָדְכוֹן יָת חֲצָדָא דְאַרְעֲכוֹן לָא תְשֵׁיצֵי פָּאתָא דְחַקְלָךְ לְמֶחְצָד וּלְקָטָא דַחֲצָדָךְ לָא תְלַקֵּט:


When you will reap the harvest of your land, you shall not completely reap a corner of your field, and the gleanings of your harvest you shall not gather.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:10)


וְכַרְמָךְ לָא תְעָלֵל וְנִתְרָא דְכַרְמָךְ לָא תְלַקֵּט לְעַנְיֵי וּלְגִיּוֹרֵי תִּשְׁבּוֹק יָתְהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


You shall not glean your vineyard, and the fallen grapes of your vineyard, you shall not gather. For the poor and the proselyte you shall leave them, I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:11)


לָא תִּגְנְבוּן וְלָא תְכַדְּבוּן וְלָא תְשַׁקְּרוּן אֱנַשׁ בְּחַבְרֵיהּ:


You shall not steal. You shall not deny [a rightful claim] and each one of you shall not lie to each other.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:12)


וְלָא תִשָּׁבְעוּן בִּשְׁמִי לְשִׁקְרָא וְתַחֵל יָת שְׁמָא דֶאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ:


You shall not swear falsely by My Name profaning the Name of your God, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:13)


לָא תַעֲשׁוֹק יָת חַבְרָךְ וְלָא **תְאַנֵּס** לָא **תְבִית** אַגְרָא דַאֲגִירָא לְוָתָךְ עַד צַפְרָא:


You shall not withhold the wages of your fellow [Jew] and you shall not rob [**oppress**]. It shall not remain overnight, [**You shall not withhold**] the wages of a day-laborer, keeping it in your possession until morning.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:14)


לָא תְלוּט **דְּלָא שָׁמַע** וְקֳדָם **דְּלָא חָזֵי** לָא תְשִׂים תַּקְלָא וְתִדְחַל מֵאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ:


You shall not curse a deaf person [**who can not hear**] and before a blind person [**who can not see**] you shall not place a stumbling-block; you shall fear your God, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:15)


לָא תַעְבְּדוּן **שְׁקַר** בְּדִינָא לָא תִסַּב אַפֵּי מַסְכֵּנָא וְלָא תֶהְדַּר אַפֵּי רַבָּא **בְּקוּשְׁטָא** תְּדִינֵיהּ לְחַבְרָךְ:


You shall not commit injustice [**lie**] in judgement, you shall not favor a poor man and you shall not show honor to a powerful man. With righteousness [**truth**] shall you judge your fellow [Jew].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:16)


לָא **תֵיכוּל קוּרְצִין** בְּעַמָּךְ לָא תְקוּם עַל דְּמָא דְחַבְרָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ:


Do not be a tale-bearer [**’eat and wink’**] among your people. You shall not stand idly by [when] the blood (life) of your fellow [is in danger]. I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:17)


לָא תִשְׂנֵי יָת אֲחוּךְ בְּלִבָּךְ אוֹכָחָא תוֹכַח יָת חַבְרָךְ וְלָא תְקַבֵּל עַל דִּי לֵיהּ חוֹבָא:


You shall not hate your brother in your heart; You shall certainly rebuke your friend; [but] you shall not bear a sin on his account.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:18)


לָא תִקּוֹם וְלָא תִטַּר דְּבָבוּ לִבְנֵי עַמָּךְ וּתְרַחֵם לְחַבְרָךְ כְּוָתָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ:


You shall not take revenge or bear a grudge against the children of your people. You shall love your fellow [Jew] as yourself, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:19)


יָת קְיָמַי תִּטְּרוּן בְּעִירָךְ לָא תַרְכֵּב עֵרוּבִין חַקְלָךְ לָא תִזְרַע עֵרוּבִין וּלְבוּשׁ עֵרוּבִין שַׁעַטְנֵזָא לָא יִסַּק עֲלָךְ:


Keep My statutes; Do not cross-breed your animals with different species. Do not sow your field with a mixture of different seeds. A garment that contains *shatnez* [a forbidden mixture of linen and wool] you shall not wear.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:20)


וּגְבַר אֲרֵי יִשְׁכּוּב עִם אִתְּתָא שִׁכְבַת זַרְעָא וְהִיא אַמְתָא אֲחִידָא לִגְבַר וְאִתְפְּרָקָא לָא אִתְפְּרִיקַת **בְּכַסְפָּא** אוֹ חֵרוּתָא לָא אִתְיְהִיבַת לַהּ **בִּשְׁטָר** בִּקֻּרְתָּא תְהֵי לָא יוּמְתוּן אֲרֵי לָא אִתְחָרָרַת:


If a man lies with a woman in a conjugal manner, and she is a maid-servant designated for a [specific] man and she has certainly not been redeemed [**with money**], or her freedom was not given to her [**through a document**]; there shall be an investigation they shall not be put to death, for her freedom was not given to her.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:21)


וְיַיְתִי יָת אַשְׁמֵיהּ **קֳדָם** יְיָ לִתְרַע מַשְׁכַּן זִמְנָא דְּכַר לַאֲשָׁמָא:


He shall bring his guilt-offering to [**before**] Adonoy, to the entrance of the Tent of Meeting, a guilt-offering-ram.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:22)


וִיכַפֵּר עֲלוֹהִי כַהֲנָא בְּדִכְרָא דַאֲשָׁמָא קֳדָם יְיָ עַל חוֹבְתֵיהּ דִּי חָב וְיִשְׁתְּבֵק לֵיהּ מֵחוֹבְתֵיהּ דִּי חָב:


The *kohein* shall atone for him with the guilt-offering-ram before Adonoy for the sin which he committed and he will be forgiven for the sin which he committed.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:23)


וַאֲרֵי תֵעֲלוּן לְאַרְעָא וְתִצְּבוּן כָּל אִלַּן דְּמֵיכַל **וּתְרַחֲקוּן** רָחָקָא יָת אִבֵּיהּ תְּלַת שְׁנִין יְהֵי לְכוֹן **מְרָחָק** **לַאֲבָדָא** לָא יִתְאֲכֵל:


When you will enter the land and you will plant any food-bearing tree, you shall regard its fruit as closed off [**separated**]. [For] three years it shall be closed off [**separated**] to you [**to be destroyed**], it shall not be eaten.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:24)


וּבְשַׁתָּא רְבִיעֵתָא יְהֵי כָּל אִבֵּיהּ קֹדֶשׁ תֻּשְׁבְּחָן **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


On the fourth year, all of its fruit shall be holy [upon which to say] praise[s to] [**before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:25)


וּבְשַׁתָּא חֲמִשֵּׁתָא תֵּיכְלוּן יָת אִבֵּיהּ לְאוֹסָפָא לְכוֹן עֲלַלְתֵּיהּ אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


On the fifth year, you may eat its fruit, so that it will increase its produce for you, I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:26)


לָא תֵיכְלוּן עַל דְּמָא לָא תְנַחֲשׁוּן וְלָא תְעוֹנוּן:


You shall not eat [sacred offerings] [before] the blood [is sprinkled on the altar]. You shall not act on the basis of omens nor act on the basis of auspicious times.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:27)


לָא תַקְּפוּן פָּאתָא דְרֵישְׁכוֹן וְלָא תְחַבֵּל יָת פָּאתָא דְדִקְנָךְ:


Do not round the corners of [the hair on] your heads and you shall not destroy [by shaving] the edges of your beard.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:28)


וְחִבּוּל עַל מִית לָא תִתְּנוּן בְּבִסְרְיכוֹן וְרוּשְׁמִין חֲרִיתִין לָא תִתְּנוּן בְּכוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ:


Gashes for the dead do not make in your skin. Do not put tattoo marks on yourselves, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:29)


לָא תַחֵל יָת בְּרַתָּךְ **לְאַטְעֲיוּתַהּ** וְלָא תִטְעֵי אַרְעָא וְתִתְמְלֵי אַרְעָא **עֵצַת חִטְאִין**:


You shall not defile your daughter by causing her to engage in harlotry [**err**] lest the land be turned astray, and the land be filled with lewdness [**a sinful plan**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:30)


יָת **יוֹמֵי** שַׁבַּיָּא דִילִי תִּטְרוּן **וּלְבֵית** מַקְדְּשִׁי תְּהוֹן דַּחֲלִין אֲנָא יְיָ:


You shall keep [**the days of**] My Shabbosos and you shall fear [**the House of**] My Sanctuary, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:31)


לָא תִתְפְּנוּן בָּתַר בִּדִּין וּדְכוּרוּ לָא תִתְבְּעוּן לְאִסְתָּאָבָא בְּהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


You shall not turn to mediums nor seek out oracles; do not seek [them] out, so as to defile yourselves through them. I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:32)


מִן קֳדָם **דְּסַבַּר בְּאוֹרַיְתָא** תְּקוּם וּתְהַדַּר אַפֵּי סָבָא וְתִדְחַל מֵאֱלָהָךְ אֲנָא יְיָ:


In the presence of the elderly [**one who understands the Torah**] you shall rise and you shall respect an elder; you shall have fear of your God, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:33)


וַאֲרֵי **יִתְגַּיַּר** עִמָּךְ גִּיּוֹרָא בְּאַרְעֲכוֹן לָא תוֹנוּן יָתֵיהּ:


If a proselyte comes to live [**converts**] with you, in your land, do not cause him anguish.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:34)


**כְּיַצִּיבָא** מִנְּכוֹן יְהֵי לְכוֹן גִּיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיַּר** בֵּינֵיכוֹן וּתְרַחֵם לֵיהּ כְּוָתָךְ אֲרֵי **דַיָּרִין** הֲוֵיתוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


As one of your native born [**settled ones**] he shall be considered by you, the proselyte who lives with [**converts among**] you, and you shall love him as yourself, for you were strangers [**foreign resident**] in the land of Egypt, I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:35)


לָא תַעְבְּדוּן **שְׁקַר** בְּדִין בִּמְשַׁחְתָּא בְּמַתְקְלָא וּבִמְכִלְתָּא:


You shall not commit injustice [**lie**] in judgement; in [land] measure, in weight or in volume measure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:36)


מֹאזְנָוָן דִּקְשׁוֹט מַתְקְלָן דִּקְשׁוֹט **מְכִילָן דִּקְשׁוֹט וְהִינִין דִּקְשׁוֹט** יְהוֹן לְכוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דִּי אַפֵּקִית יָתְכוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


Just scales and just weights a just dry measure (*ephah*) [**just volume measures**] and a just liquid measure (*hin*) [**just hinin**] you shall have for yourselves, I am Adonoy, your God who has taken you out of the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 19:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/19:37)


וְתִטְּרוּן יָת כָּל קְיָמַי וְיָת כָּל דִּינַי וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָתְהוֹן אֲנָא יְיָ:


You must keep all my statutes and all of my laws, and do [observe] them. I am Adonoy.



## Chapter 20

###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:1)

Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying:


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:2)


וְעִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל תֵּימַר גְּבַר גְּבַר מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִין גִּיּוֹרָא **דְּיִתְגַּיְרוּן** בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל דִּי יִתֵּן מִזַּרְעֵיהּ לְמוֹלֶךְ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל עַמָּא **בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל** יִרְגְּמֻנֵּיהּ בְּאַבְנָא:


And to Bnei Yisrael you shall say: that each and every man of Bnei Yisroel and from the proselytes that reside [**convert**] in Israel, who will give any of his children to *Molech*, [that] he shall surely be put to death; the people of the land [**House of Israel**] shall stone him to death.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:3)


וַאֲנָא אֶתֵּן יָת רוּגְזִי בְּגַבְרָא הַהוּא וֶאֱשֵׁיצֵי יָתֵיהּ מִגּוֹ עַמֵּיהּ אֲרֵי מִזַּרְעֵיהּ יְהַב לְמוֹלֶךְ בְּדִיל לְסָאֲבָא יָת מַקְדְּשִׁי וּלְאַחַלָּא יָת שְׁמָא דְקוּדְשִׁי:


I shall direct My wrath on that man and I shall cut him off from among his people, for he gave of his children to *Molech*, in order to defile My Sanctuary and profane My Holy Name.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:4)


וְאִם מִכְבַּשׁ יִכְבְּשׁוּן עַמָּא **בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל** יָת עֵינֵיהוֹן מִן גַּבְרָא הַהוּא בִּדְיָהֵב מִזַּרְעֵיהּ לְמוֹלֶךְ בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְקַטָּלָא יָתֵיהּ:


If the people of the land [**House of Israel**] will hide their eyes from that person, when he gives of his children to *Molech*, and they do not put him to death.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:5)


וֶאֱשַׁוֵּי אֲנָא יָת רוּגְזִי בְּגַבְרָא הַהוּא **וּבִסְעָדוֹהִי** וֶאֱשֵׁיצֵי יָתֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל דְּטָעָן בַּתְרוֹהִי לְמִטְעֵי בָּתַר מוֹלֶךְ מִגּוֹ עַמְּהוֹן:


I shall set My wrath against that man and his family [**supporters**]; I shall cut him off, along with all those who go astray after him to go astray after *Molech*, [they will be cut off] from among their people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:6)

The person who turns to mediums and oracles to stray after them; I shall direct My wrath against that person and cut him off from among his people.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:7)


וְתִתְקַדְּשׁוּן וּתְהוֹן קַדִּישִׁין אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


You shall sanctify yourselves and be holy for I am Adonoy, your God.

You must keep My statutes and do [observe] them. I am Adonoy who sanctifies you.

אֲרֵי גְבַר גְּבַר דִּי יְלוּט יָת אֲבוּהִי וְיָת אִמֵּיהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל אֲבוּהִי וְאִמֵּיהּ לַט **קְטָלָא חַיָּב**:


Any man that curses his father or his mother shall surely be put to death. [Since] he has cursed his father or mother, his blood is on him [**he is liable to the death penalty**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:10)


וּגְבַר דִּי יְגוּף יָת אִתַּת גְּבַר דִּי יְגוּף יָת אִתַּת חַבְרֵיהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל גַּיָּפָא וְגַיַּפְתָּא:


A man who commits adultery with another mans wife who commits adultery with the wife of his fellow man—they shall surely be put to death—the adulterer and the adulteress.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:11)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִשְׁכּוּב עִם אִתַּת אֲבוּהִי עֶרְיְתָא דַאֲבוּהִי גַּלִּי אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקַטְּלוּן תַּרְוֵיהוֹן **קְטָלָא חַיָּבִין**:


A man who will lie [conjugally] with his fathers wife has uncovered his fathers nakedness; they both shall surely be put to death, their blood is on them [**they are liable to the death penalty**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:12)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִשְׁכּוּב יָת כַּלְּתֵיהּ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקַטְּלוּן תַּרְוֵיהוֹן תִּבְלָא עֲבָדוּ **קְטָלָא חַיָּבִין**:


A man who will lie [conjugally] with his daughter-in-law; they both shall surely be put to death they have committed an act of perversion, their blood is on them [**they are liable to the death penalty**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:13)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִשְׁכּוּב יָת דְּכוּרָא מִשְׁכְּבֵי אִתְּתָא תּוֹעֶבְתָּא עֲבָדוּ תַּרְוֵיהוֹן אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקַטְּלוּן **קְטָלָא חַיָּבִין**:


A man who will lie [conjugally] with [another] man, in the same manner as with a woman; they both have committed an abominable act, they shall surely be put to death, their blood is on them [**they are liable to the death penalty**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:14)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִסַּב יָת אִתְּתָא וְיָת אִמַּהּ **עֵצַת חִטְאִין** הִיא בְּנוּרָא יוֹקְדוּן יָתֵיהּ וְיָתְהֶן וְלָא תְהֵי **עֵצַת חִטְאִין** בֵּינֵיכוֹן:


A man who marries a woman and her mother; it is lewdness [**a sinful plan**]. They shall be burned in fire, he and [one of] them, and there will no longer be such lewdness [**a sinful plan**] among you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:15)

A man who lies [conjugally] with an animal shall surely be put to death, and you shall also kill the animal.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:16)


וְאִתְּתָא דִּי תִקְרַב לְכָל בְּעִירָא **לְמִשְׁלַט בַּהּ** וְתִקְטוֹל יָת אִתְּתָא וְיָת בְּעִירָא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקַטְּלוּן **קְטָלָא חַיָּבִין**:


A woman who presents herself to an animal causing it to copulate with her [**control her**]; you shall kill the woman and the animal. They shall surely be killed, their blood is on them [**they are liable to the death penalty**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:17)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִסַּב יָת אֲחָתֵיהּ בַּת אֲבוּהִי אוֹ בַת אִמֵּיהּ וְיֶחֱזֵי יָת עֶרְיְתַהּ וְהִיא תֶחֱזֵי יָת עֶרְיְתֵיהּ קְלָנָא הוּא וְיִשְׁתֵּיצוּן לְעֵינֵי בְּנֵי עַמְּהוֹן עֶרְיַת אֲחָתֵיהּ גַּלִּי חוֹבֵיהּ יְקַבֵּל:


A man who marries his sister, either his fathers daughter or his mothers daughter, and he sees her nakedness and she sees his nakedness; it is a shameful perversion. They shall be cut off before their people he has uncovered the nakedness of his sister, he shall bear [the burden of] his iniquity.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:18)

A man who will lie [conjugally] with a menstruating [**an impure**] woman and uncover her nakedness and uncover her source [womb] [**shameful perversion**] and she has uncovered the source [**impurity**] of her blood; they shall both be cut off from among their people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:19)

The nakedness of your mothers sister and of your fathers sister, you shall not uncover, for the nakedness of his close kin he will have bared they shall bear [the burden of] their iniquity.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:20)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִשְׁכּוּב יָת **אִתַּת אַח אֲבוּהִי** עֶרְיַת **אַח אֲבוּהִי** גַּלִּי חוֹבְהֶן יְקַבְּלוּן בְּלָא וְלָד יְמוּתוּן:


A man who will lie [conjugally] with his aunt [**father’s brother’s wife**] has uncovered the nakedness of his uncle [**father’s brother**] they will bear [the burden of] their sin and they will die without children.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:21)


וּגְבַר דִּי יִסַּב יָת אִתַּת אֲחוּהִי מְרָחָקָא הִיא עֶרְיְתָא דַאֲחוּהִי גַּלִּי בְּלָא וְלָד יְהוֹן:


A man who will marry his brothers wife who must be [conjugally] avoided [by him]; he has uncovered his brothers nakedness, they shall die without children.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:22)


וְתִטְּרוּן יָת כָּל קְיָמַי וְיָת כָּל דִּינַי וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָתְהוֹן וְלָא תְרוֹקֵן יָתְכוֹן אַרְעָא דִּי אֲנָא מַעֵיל יָתְכוֹן תַּמָּן לְמִתַּב בַּהּ:


You shall keep all of My statutes and all of My laws and do (observe) them [so that] you will not be expelled by the land to which I am bringing you there to reside therein.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:23)

You shall not follow the customs of the nation which I am expelling before you. For they have done all these above mentioned sins and I became disgusted with [**my Word distanced**] them.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:24)


וַאֲמָרִית לְכוֹן אַתּוּן תִּירְתוּן יָת אַרְעֲהוֹן וַאֲנָא אֶתְּנִנַּהּ לְכוֹן לְמֵירַת יָתַהּ אַרְעָא **עָבְדָא** חֲלָב וּדְבָשׁ אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דִּי אַפְרֵשִׁית יָתְכוֹן מִן עַמְמַיָּא:


And I have said to you [that] you shall inherit their land, and I shall give it to you that you may inherit it, a land flowing with [**producing**] milk and honey, I am Adonoy, your God who separated you from the [other] peoples.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:25)


וְתַפְרְשׁוּן בֵּין בְּעִירָא דַכְיָא לִמְסָאָבָא וּבֵין עוֹפָא מְסָאָבָא לִדְכֵי וְלָא תְשַׁקְּצוּן יָת נַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן בִּבְעִירָא וּבְעוֹפָא וּבְכֹל דִּי תַרְחֵשׁ אַרְעָא דִּי אַפְרֵשִׁית לְכוֹן לְסָאָבָא:


You shall distinguish between a [ritually] clean animal and a unclean one, between the unclean fowl and the clean one. Do not make yourselves disgusting through the animals, fowl or through any creature that creeps on the ground which I have distinguished for you as being unclean.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:26)


וּתְהוֹן **קֳדָמַי** קַדִּישִׁין אֲרֵי קַדִּישׁ אֲנָא יְיָ וְאַפְרֵשִׁית יָתְכוֹן מִן עַמְמַיָּא לְמֶהֱוֵי **פָלְחִין קֳדָמָי**:


You shall be holy to [**before**] Me, for I, Adonoy, am holy and I have distinguished you from the [other] peoples to be Mine [and to serve Me] [**be worshippers before Me**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 20:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/20:27)


וּגְבַר אוֹ אִתְּתָא אֲרֵי יְהֵי בְהוֹן בִּדִּין אוֹ דְּכוּר אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקַטְּלוּן בְּאַבְנָא יִרְגְּמוּן יָתְהוֹן **קְטָלָא חַיָּבִין**:


If among the men or women there will be a medium or an oracle they shall surely be put to death. You shall stone them to death, their blood is on them [**they are liable to the death penalty**].



## Chapter 21

###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְמשֶׁה אֱמַר לְכַהֲנַיָּא בְּנֵי אַהֲרֹן וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן עַל מִית לָא יִסְתָּאַב בְּעַמֵּיהּ:


Adonoy said to Moshe; speak, to the *kohanim*, the sons of Aharon, and you shall say to them, let him not defile himself with the dead among his people.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:2)


אֶלָּהֵן לְקָרִיבֵיהּ דְּקָרִיב לֵיהּ לְאִמֵּיהּ וְלַאֲבוּהִי וְלִבְרֵיהּ וְלִבְרַתֵּיהּ וְלַאֲחוּהִי:


Except to his kin to whom he is closely related, for his mother and his father, for his son, his daughter and for his brother.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:3)


וְלַאֲחָתֵיהּ בְּתֻלְתָּא דְּקָרִיבָא לֵיהּ דִּי לָא הֲוַת לִגְבָר לַהּ יִסְתָּאָב:


For his virgin sister, to whom he is closely related, who has not yet been married, for her, he may be defiled.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:4)


לָא יִסְתָּאַב **רַבָּא בְעַמֵּיהּ** לְאַחֲלוּתֵיהּ:


A husband [**An honored one among his people**] may not defile himself among his people [for a wife through whom] he becomes profaned.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:5)


לָא יִמְרְטוּן מְרַט בְּרֵישְׁהוֹן וּפָאתָא דְדִקְנְהוֹן לָא יְגַלְּחוּן וּבְבִשְׂרְהוֹן לָא יְחַבְּלוּן חִבּוּל:


They shall not make baldness on their heads and the corners of their beards they shall not shave, and they shall not make gashes in their flesh.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:6)


קַדִּישִׁין יְהוֹן **קֳדָם** אֱלָהֲהוֹן וְלָא יְחַלּוּן שְׁמָא דֶּאֱלָהֲהוֹן אֲרֵי יָת **קֻרְבְּנַיָּא** דַיְיָ **קֻרְבָּן** אֱלָהֲהוֹן אִנּוּן מַקְרִיבִין וִיהוֹן קַדִּישִׁין:


They shall be holy to [**before**] their God and they shall not profane the Name of their God, for [it is] the fire-offerings [**offerings**] of Adonoy, the bread [**sacrifice**] of their God [that] they bring [offer], and they must [therefore] be holy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:7)


אִתְּתָא **מַטְעֲיָא** וּמְחַלְּלָא לָא יִסְּבוּן וְאִתְּתָא דִּמְתָרְכָא מִבַּעְלַהּ לָא יִסְּבוּן אֲרֵי קַדִּישׁ הוּא **קֳדָם** אֱלָהֵיהּ:


A harlot [**One who strays**] or a profaned woman they shall not marry. A woman divorced from her husband they shall not marry, for he is holy to [**before**] his God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:8)


וּתְקַדְּשִׁנֵּיהּ אֲרֵי יָת **קֻרְבַּן** אֱלָהָךְ הוּא מְקָרֵב קַדִּישׁ יְהֵי לָךְ אֲרֵי קַדִּישׁ אֲנָא יְיָ מְקַדִּשְׁכוֹן:


You shall make him holy for he offers the bread [**sacrifice**] of your God; he shall be holy unto you for I, Adonoy, am holy, Who makes you holy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:9)


וּבַת גְּבַר כָּהֵן אֲרֵי תִתְחַל **לְמִטְעֵי מִקְּדֻשַּׁת אֲבוּהָ הִיא מִתַּחֲלָא** בְּנוּרָא תִּתּוֹקָד:


The daughter of a *kohein*, who profanes herself by fornication [**straying**]—she profanes her father—[**she becomes profane with respect to her father’s holiness**] she shall be burned with fire.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:10)


וְכַהֲנָא דְאִתְרַבָּא מֵאֲחוֹהִי דְּ יִתָּרִיק עַל רֵישֵׁיהּ מִשְׁחָא **דִרְבוּתָא** וְדִי **קָרִיב יָת קֻרְבָּנֵיהּ** לְמִלְבַּשׁ יָת לְבוּשַׁיָּא יָת רֵישֵׁיהּ לָא יְרַבֵּי פֵרוּעַ וּלְבוּשׁוֹהִי לָא יִבְזַע:


The *kohein* who is greatest among his brethren upon whose head was poured the anointing oil [**which inaugurates to greatness**]; whose hands [**sacrifices**] were consecrated [**offered**] to put on the special garments; shall not let his hair grow wild and he shall not tear his garments.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:11)


וְעַל כָּל נַפְשַׁת מֵתָא לָא יֵיעוֹל לַאֲבוּהִי וּלְאִמֵּיהּ לָא יִסְתָּאָב:


And adjacent to any dead body he shall not enter. For his father and mother he shall not become [ritually] impure.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:12)


וּמִין מַקְדְּשָׁא לָא יִפּוֹק וְלָא יַחֵל יָת מַקְדְּשָׁא דֶאֱלָהֵיהּ אֲרֵי כְּלִיל מְשַׁח **רְבוּתָא** דֶאֱלָהֵיהּ עֲלוֹהִי אֲנָא יְיָ:


He shall not depart from the Sanctuary and he shall not profane his Gods Sanctuary, for the crown of his Gods anointing oil [**which inaugurates to greatness**] is upon him, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:13)


וְהוּא אִתְּתָא בִּבְתֻלְתָּהָא יִסָּב:


He shall marry [only] a virgin.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:14)


אַרְמְלָא וּמְתָרְכָא וְחַלִּילָא **מַטְעֲיָא** יָת אִלֵּין לָא יִסָּב אֱלָהֵן בְּתֻלְתָּא מֵעַמֵּיהּ יִסַּב אִתְּתָא:


A widow, divorcee, profaned woman or harlot [**one who strays**]; he may not marry [any] of these. But only a virgin from his own people may he marry.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:15)


וְלָא יַחֵל זַרְעֵיהּ בְּעַמֵּיהּ אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ מְקַדְּשֵׁיהּ:


He shall not profane his offspring among his people, for I am Adonoy, who makes him holy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:16)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:17)


מַלֵּל עִם אַהֲרֹן לְמֵימָר גְּבַר מִבְּנָיךְ לְדָרֵיהוֹן דִּי יְהֵי בֵיהּ מוּמָא לָא יִקְרַב לְקָרָבָא **קֻרְבָּנָא קֳדָם** אֱלָהֵיהּ:


Speak to Aharon saying; Anyone of your offspring, through [all] their generations who will have a blemish, shall not approach to offer the bread [**sacrifice**] of [**before**] his God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:18)


אֲרֵי כָל גְּבַר דִּי בֵיהּ מוּמָא לָא יִקְרָב גְּבַר עַוִּיר אוֹ חֲגִיר אוֹ חָרִים אוֹ שְׂרִיעַ:


For any man who has a blemish shall not approach; a blind man or a lame man, or a man with a sunken nose, or a man who has disproportionate limbs.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:19)


אוֹ גְבַר דִּי יְהֵי בֵיהּ תְּבַר רַגְלָא אוֹ תְּבַר יְדָא:


or a man who has a broken (crippled) foot, or a broken (crippled) hand,


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:20)


אוֹ גְבִין אוֹ דֻקָּא אוֹ חִלִּיז בְּעֵינֵיהּ אוֹ גַרְבָן אוֹ חֲזָזָן אוֹ מְרַס פַּחֲדִין:


or one with overhanging eyebrows, or with a cataract, or a disarrangement [in the eye], or one with scurf or scabs, or one with crushed testicles.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:21)


כָּל גְּבַר דִּי בֵיהּ מוּמָא מִזַּרְעָא דְּאַהֲרֹן כַּהֲנָא לָא יִקְרַב לְקָרָבָא יָת **קֻרְבָּנַיָּא** דַיְיָ מוּמָא בֵיהּ יָת **קֻרְבַּן** אֱלָהֵיהּ לָא יִקְרַב לְקָרָבָא:


Any man who has a blemish, who is a descendant of Aharon the *kohein*, shall not approach to offer the fire-offerings [**offerings**] of Adonoy. He has a blemish; the bread [**sacrifice**] of his God he may not approach to offer [as an offering].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:22)


קֻרְבַּן אֱלָהֵיהּ מִקֹּדֶשׁ קוּדְשַׁיָּא וּמִן קוּדְשַׁיָּא יֵיכוּל:


The bread of his God which is of the most holy, and from the holy [offerings] he may eat.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:23)


בְּרַם לְפָרֻכְתָּא לָא יֵיעוֹל וּלְמַדְבְּחָא לָא יִקְרַב אֲרֵי מוּמָא בֵיהּ וְלָא יַחֵל יָת מַקְדְּשַׁי אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ מְקַדִּשְׁהוֹן:


However, he may not enter beyond the Curtain and he shall not approach the Altar for he has a blemish and he shall not profane My holy things, for I am Adonoy, who sanctifies them.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/21:24)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם אַהֲרֹן וְעִם בְּנוֹהִי וְעִם כָּל בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Moshe spoke [these words] to Aharon, and to his sons and to all of Bnei Yisroel.



## Chapter 22

###### Onkelos Leviticus 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/22:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe, saying;


###### Onkelos Leviticus 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/22:2)


מַלֵּיל עִם אַהֲרֹן וְעִם בְּנוֹהִי וְיִפָּרְשׁוּן מִקּוּדְשַׁיָּא דִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְלָא יְחַלּוּן יָת שְׁמָא דְקוּדְשִׁי דִּי אִנּוּן מְקַדְּשִׁין **קֳדָמַי** אֲנָא יְיָ:


Speak to Aharon and to his sons and let them keep away from the holy offerings of Bnei Yisroel and they shall not profane My holy name which they sanctify for [**before**] Me, I am Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/22:3)

Say to them; throughout your generations; any person of your offspring who will draw near to the holy things which Bnei Yisroel will sanctify to [**before**] Adonoy [while] his impurity is on him that soul [**person**] shall be cut off from My Presence, I am Adonoy.

## Chapter 23
