###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:15)


וְעִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּמַלֵּל לְמֵימָר גְּבַר גְּבַר דִּי **יַרְגֵּז** קֳדָם אֱלָהֵיהּ וִיקַבֵּל חוֹבֵיהּ:


And to Bnei Yisroel you shall speak, saying; Any person who shall blaspheme [**cause anger before**] his God shall bear [the burden of] his sin.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:16)


וְדִי פָרֵשׁ שְׁמָא דַיְיָ אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל מִרְגַּם יִרְגְּמוּן בֵּיהּ כָּל כְּנִשְׁתָּא כְּגִיּוֹרָא **כְּיַצִּיבָא** בְּפָרָשׁוּתֵיהּ שְׁמָא יִתְקְטֵל:


Whoever pronounces clearly the Name of Adonoy [and blasphemes] he will surely be put to death. The entire congregation shall surely stone him. The proselyte and native born [**settled one**] alike when he clearly articulates the Name, shall be put to death.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:17)


וּגְבַר אֲרֵי יִקְטוֹל כָּל נַפְשָׁא דַאֲנָשָׁא אִתְקְטָלָא יִתְקְטֵל:


Any man who kills any human being must surely be put to death.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:18)


וּדְיִקְטוֹל נְפַשׁ בְּעִירָא יְשַׁלְּמִנַּהּ נַפְשָׁא חֲלַף נַפְשָׁא:


Whoever kills an animal shall pay for it, [the compensation of] an animal for an animal.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:19)


וּגְבַר אֲרֵי יִתֵּן מוּמָא בְּחַבְרֵיהּ כְּמָא דִי עֲבַד כֵּן יִתְעֲבֶד לֵיהּ:


A man who maims his neighbor just as he did, so shall be done to him.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:20)


תַּבְרָא חֳלַף תַּבְרָא עֵינָא חֳלַף עֵינָא שִׁנָּא חֳלַף שִׁנָּא כְּמָא דִי יִתֵּן מוּמָא בַּאֲנָשָׁא כֵּן יִתְיְהֶב בֵּיהּ:


[Compensation for] a broken bone [in exchange] for a broken bone, [compen-sation for] the loss of an eye [in exchange] for the loss of an eye, [compensation for] the loss to a tooth [in exchange] for the loss of a tooth; just as he maimed the man, so shall it be done to him [monetarily].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:21)


**וּדְיִקְטוֹל** בְּעִירָא יְשַׁלְּמִנַּהּ **וּדְיִקְטוֹל** אֲנָשָׁא יִתְקְטֵל:


Whoever smites [**kills**] an animal shall pay for it. Whoever smites [**kills**] a person shall be put to death.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:22)


דִּינָא חֲדָא יְהֵי לְכוֹן כְּגִיּוֹרָא **כְיַצִּיבָא** יְהֵי אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


One rule shall apply for all of you just as it is for the proselyte, so it shall be for the native born [**settled one**], for I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/24:23)


וּמַלִּיל משֶׁה עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאַפִּיקוּ יָת **דְּאַרְגֵּז** לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא וּרְגָמוּ יָתֵיהּ אַבְנָא וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עֲבָדוּ כְּמָא דִי פַקֵּיד יְיָ יָת משֶׁה:


Moshe spoke to Bnei Yisroel and they took the blasphemer [**one who caused anger**], beyond the encampment and they stoned him with a stone. And Bnei Yisroel did just as Adonoy commanded Moshe.



## Chapter 25

###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:1)


וּמַלִּיל יְיָ עִם משֶׁה בְּטוּרָא דְסִינַי לְמֵימָר:


Adonoy spoke to Moshe on Mount Sinai, saying;


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:2)


מַלֵּיל עִם בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן אֲרֵי תֵעֲלוּן לְאַרְעָא דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב לְכוֹן וְתַשְׁמֵט אַרְעָא שְׁמִטְּתָא קֳדָם יְיָ:


Speak to Bnei Yisroel and say to them, when you come to the land which I give to you the land shall be at rest—a *Shabbos* for [**rest before**] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:3)


שִׁית שְׁנִין תִּזְרַע חַקְלָךְ וְשִׁית שְׁנִין תִּכְסַח כַּרְמָךְ וְתִכְנוֹשׁ יָת עֲלַלְתַּהּ:


For six years you shall plant your field and for six years you shall prune your vineyard and you shall harvest its produce.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:4)


וּבְשַׁתָּא שְׁבִיעֲתָא נְיַח שְׁמִטְּתָא יְהֵי לְאַרְעָא דְּתַשְׁמֵט קֳדָם יְיָ חַקְלָךְ לָא תִזְרַע וְכַרְמָךְ לָא תִכְסָח:


But the seventh year shall be a *Shabbos* of [**complete**] rest for the land, a *Shabbos* for [**rest before**] Adonoy, you shall not plant your field and you shall not prune your vineyard.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:5)


יָת כַּתֵּי דַחֲצָדָךְ לָא תֶחֱצַד וְיָת עִנְּבֵי שִׁבְקָךְ לָא תִקְטוֹף שְׁנַת שְׁמִטְּתָא יְהֵי לְאַרְעָא:


[Even] crops that grew on their own [from the seeds of your previous] harvest you shall not reap, and the grapes of your untended vines you shall not gather; it shall be a year of complete rest for the land.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:6)


וּתְהֵי שְׁמִטַּת אַרְעָא לְכוֹן לְמֵיכַל לָךְ וּלְעַבְדָּךְ וּלְאַמְתָךְ וְלַאֲגִירָךְ וּלְתוֹתָבָךְ דְּדָיְרִין עִמָּךְ:


The [produce] [**rest**] of the lands *Shabbos*[-year] [**land**] shall be for yourselves, for food, for you, your servant and your maidservant, for your hired hand and your resident sojourner who reside with you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:7)


וְלִבְעִירָךְ וּלְחַיְתָא דִּי בְאַרְעָךְ תְּהֵי כָל עֲלַלְתַּהּ לְמֵיכָל:


[Also] for your domesticated animals and for the [wild] beasts that are in your land shall all of its produce be for food.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:8)


וְתִמְנֵי לָךְ שְׁבַע שְׁמִטִּין דִּשְׁנִין שְׁבַע שְׁנִין שְׁבַע זִמְנִין וִיהוֹן לָךְ יוֹמֵי שְׁבַע שְׁמִטִּין דִּשְׁנִין אַרְבְּעִין וְתֵשַׁע שְׁנִין:


You shall count for yourself seven sabbatical years [**of rest**], seven years, seven times and it shall be for you, the days (period) of the seven sabbatical years [**of rest**], forty-nine years.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:9)


וְתַעֲבַר שׁוֹפַר יַבָּבָא בְּיַרְחָא שְׁבִיעָאָה בְּעַשְׂרָא לְיַרְחָא בְּיוֹמָא דְכִפּוּרַיָּא תַּעַבְּרוּן שׁוֹפָרָא בְּכָל אַרְעֲכוֹן:


You shall make a proclamation [**crying**] with the *shofor* on the tenth day of the seventh month. On Yom Kippur shall this *shofor*-proclamation be made. throughout all your land.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:10)


וּתְקַדְּשׁוּן יָת שְׁנַת חַמְשִׁין שְׁנִין וְתַקְרוּן חֵרוּתָא בְּאַרְעָא לְכָל יָתְבָהָא יוֹבֵלָא הִיא תְּהֵי לְכוֹן וּתְתוּבוּן גְּבַר לְאַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ וּגְבַר לְזַרְעִיתֵיהּ תְּתוּבוּן:


You shall sanctify the year of the fiftieth year and proclaim freedom in the land for all of its inhabitants; it shall be for you a jubilee and each man shall return to his ancestral land and each man shall return to his family.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:11)


יוֹבֵלָא הִיא שְׁנַת חַמְשִׁין שְׁנִין תְּהֵי לְכוֹן לָא תִזְרְעוּן וְלָא תַחְצְדוּן יָת כַּתָּהָא וְלָא תִקְטְפוּן יָת שִׁבְקָהָא:


It is a jubilee, [that] the year of the fiftieth year shall be for you. You shall not plant and you shall not reap [even] crops that grew on their own; and you shall not gather the [grapes of] its untended vineyard,


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:12)


אֲרֵי יוֹבֵלָא הִיא קוּדְשָׁא תְהֵי לְכוֹן מִן חַקְלָא תֵּיכְלוּן יָת עֲלַלְתַּהּ:


For it is a jubilee, it shall be holy to you. Whatever is in the field, you may eat of its produce.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:13)


בְּשַׁתָּא דְיוֹבֵלָא הָדָא תְּתוּבוּן גְּבַר לְאַחֲסַנְתֵּיהּ:


On this jubilee year, each man shall return to his ancestral land.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:14)


וַאֲרֵי תְזַבֵּן זְבִינִין לְחַבְרָךְ אוֹ תִזְבֵּן מִידָא דְחַבְרָךְ לָא תוֹנוּן גְּבַר יָת אֲחוֹהִי:


If you sell anything to your neighbor, or purchase [something] from your neighbor, do not cheat one another.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:15)


בְּמִנְיַן שְׁנַיָּא בָּתַר יוֹבֵלָא תִּזְבֵּן מִן חַבְרָךְ בְּמִנְיַן שְׁנֵי עֲלַלְתָּא יְזַבֶּן לָךְ:


According to the number of years after the jubilee you shall purchase [land] from your neighbor; according to the number of produce years he shall sell it to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:16)


לְפוּם סְגִיאוּת שְׁנַיָּא תַּסְגֵּי זְבִינוֹהִי וּלְפוּם זְעֵרוּת שְׁנַיָּא תַּזְעֵר זְבִינוֹהִי אֲרֵי מִנְיַן עֲלַלְתָּא הוּא מְזַבֵּן לָךְ:


Based on the abundance of years, you shall increase its purchase price and based on the lack of years you shall decrease its purchase price, since it is the number of produce-seasons that he is selling to you.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:17)


וְלָא תוֹנוּן גְּבַר יָת חַבְרֵיהּ וְתִדְחַל מֵאֱלָהָךְ אֲרֵי אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן:


You shall not cheat one another, and you shall fear your God for I am Adonoy, your God.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:18)


וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָת קְיָמַי וְיָת דִּינַי תִּטְּרוּן וְתַעְבְּדוּן יָתְהוֹן וְתֵיתְבוּן עַל אַרְעָא לְרָחְצָן:


You shall fulfill My statutes and keep My laws and fulfill them, and you will then live in the land in security.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:19)


וְתִתֵּן אַרְעָא אִבַּהּ וְתֵיכְלוּן לְמִשְׂבָּע וְתֵיתְבוּן לְרָחֲצָן עֲלַהּ:


The land will give forth its fruit and you will eat your fill, and you will live securely on it.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:20)


וַאֲרֵי תֵימְרוּן מָא נֵיכוּל בְּשַׁתָּא שְׁבִיעֵתָא הָא לָא נִזְרָע וְלָא נִכְנוֹשׁ יָת עֲלַלְתָּנָא:


If you shall say (ask) What will we eat in the seventh year, for lo! we have not planted nor gathered our produce?


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:21)


וֶאֱפַקֵּד יָת בִּרְכָתִי לְכוֹן בְּשַׁתָּא שְׁתִיתֵתָא וְתַעְבֵּד יָת עֲלַלְתָּא לִתְלַת שְׁנִין:


I shall command (direct) My blessing to you in the sixth year and it will produce [enough] for three years.


###### Onkelos Leviticus 25:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/25:22)


וְתִזְרְעוּן יָת שַׁתָּא תְמִינֵתָא וְתֵיכְלוּן מִן עֲלַלְתָּא עַתִּיקָא עַד שַׁתָּא תְשִׁיעֵתָא עַד מֵעַל עֲלַלְתַּהּ תֵּיכְלוּן עַתִּיקָא:

## Chapter 27

###### Onkelos Leviticus 27:1

###### Onkelos Leviticus 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/27:3)

The valuation for a male shall be, from twenty years of age to sixty years of age your valuation shall be fifty holy silver *shekalim* [**selaim**].


###### Onkelos Leviticus 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/27:4)

###### Onkelos Leviticus 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Leviticus/r/27:5)
